## Document 008: 10月通胀数据点评：能源链主导上游涨价，低位行业延续修复

Source PDF: all report pdf/10月通胀数据点评：能源链主导上游涨价，低位行业延续修复.pdf
Report source: 联储证券
Date: 2026-11-10
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

证券研究报告 
宏观经济点评 2026 年 11 月 10 日

请务必阅读正文之后的信息披露和法律声明

10 月通胀数据点评：能源链主导上游涨价，低位行业延续修复   
[Table_Author] 
 魏争 分析师  沈夏宜 分析师

Email:weizheng@lczq.com Email:shenxiayi@lczq.com

### Page 2

宏观经济点评  
请务必阅读正文之后的信息披露和法律声明 2 
0.7%，指向工业品价格仍处上行区间，上涨速度出现放缓。结构上看，一
是输入性能源价格继续强化，化工链价格上升。 10 月中东局势扰动仍然
较强，国际能源价格升至高位，输入性价格继续传导。石油和天然气开采
业价格同比升至 35.0%（上月 24.5%），石油煤炭加工业升至 29.9%（上
月 21.6%），化学原料制品制造业升至 16.4%（上月 12.1%），化纤制造业
16.0%（上月 12.3%）；环比看，石油和天然气开采业上涨 6.0%（上月
10.4%）。二是煤炭价格维持高增，上行斜率 放缓。煤炭开采和洗选业价
格同比升至 36.6%（上月 35.3%），环比涨幅降至 2.7%（上月 9.5%）。供
给端约束叠加高油价替代效应，煤价维持高位，但经历前期快速上涨后边
际动能有所放缓。三是，有色链由前期持续走强转为降温。 有色金属矿采
选业同比降至 16.3%（上月 21.4%），有色冶炼同比降至 18.6%（上月
21.4%）。AI 产业链下游价格维持韧性，计算机通信和其他电子设备制造
业同比由 5.8%小幅升至 5.9%，电气机械和器材制造业维持 6.2%。 
生产资料与生活资料 PPI 分化延续，部分长期负增行业继续收窄。10
月，生产资料 PPI 同比上涨 7.4%，较 9 月提高 0.9 个百分点；生活资料
PPI 同比下降 0.1%，与 9 月持平，二者分化仍然明显。细分看，一方
面，部分此前长期低迷行业延续修复 。一是建筑材料链延续改善。非金制
品业价格同比降幅收窄至 1.6%（上月 2.5%），环比涨幅扩大至 0.8%（上
月 0.3%）；黑金冶炼和压延加工业同比上涨 0.4%（上月-0.2%），指向建
筑材料链延续了 9 月以来修复趋势，或与“反内卷”以来相关行业产能调
整、供求关系改善有关。二是多数长期负增的消费制造行业同比降幅继续
收窄。汽车制造业由-2.0%收窄至-1.8%，医药制造业由-3.3%收窄至-
3.2%，酒饮料和精制茶制造业由-5.3%收窄至-5.2%，农副食品加工业由-
0.9%收窄至-0.4%。另一方面，下游价格改善仍未形成全面扩散。 生活资
料中，衣着、一般日用品价格降幅小幅扩大，耐用消费品同比涨幅回落至
1.4%（上月 1.6%）；环比看，生活资料整体增速为-0.1%（上月 0.1%）。 
往后看，上下游价格分化存在进一步收敛的条件， 广谱性修复仍需内需接
力。一方面，中期选举后美国国内对进一步扩大中东冲突的约束有所增
强，前期推动油价快速上行的地缘风险溢价或有所回落，能源价格继续大
幅上冲的概率下降，输入性通胀对 PPI 上游价格的边际支撑可能趋弱。另
一方面，建筑材料及部分低位制造业价格已连续改善，叠加四季度政策项
目逐步形成实物工作量，黑色、建材等行业仍存在进一步修复的条件 。后
续价格结构性分化有望从两端同时收敛，一端是能源等上游行业价格上行
斜率放缓，另一端是建筑链及部分长期低迷行业逐步修复。总量上看，10
月核心 CPI 和生活资料 PPI 均未同步走强，指向当前下游修复尚未形成广
泛的内生价格动能。后续判断价格向广谱改善转变仍在于两条链条，一是
黑色、建材等价格改善能否在政策需求支撑下延续并向中游制造扩散； 二
是食品加工、汽车、医药等低位行业跌幅收窄能否进一步转化为生活资料
PPI、核心 CPI 和服务价格的持续改善。

风险提示：国际油价大幅波动超预期、国内需求修复不及预期。

投资建议的评级标准  评级 说明 
评级标准为报告发布日后的 6 个月内公司股价 （或行业
指数） 相对同期基准指数的相对市场表现。 其中A 股市
场以沪深 300 指数为基准； 新三板市场以三板成指 （针
对协议转让标的） 或三板做市指数 （针对做市转让标的）
为基准；香港市场以恒生指数为基准。 
股票评级 
买入 相对同期基准指数涨幅在 10%以上 
增持 相对同期基准指数涨幅在 5%~10%之间 
中性 相对同期基准指数涨幅在-5%~+5%之间 
减持 相对同期基准指数跌幅在 5%以上 
行业评级 
看好 相对表现优于市场 
中性 相对表现与市场持平 
看淡 相对表现弱于市场

## Document 072: 2026年10月美联储议息会议点评：加息暂歇，鹰声未止

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026年10月美联储议息会议点评：加息暂歇，鹰声未止.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

宏观经济深度研究 - 2026 年 10 月 29 日 1 
证券研究 
宏观经济深度研究 
加息暂歇，鹰声未止 
——2026 年 10 月美联储议息会议点评

北京时间 10 月 29 日凌晨，美联储议息会议宣布保持联邦基金利率目标区间不
变在 3.75%—4.00%。近期美国经济仍有韧性，二季度美国实际 GDP 环比折年
增速由此前公布的 1.5%上修至 2.2%，消费和私人投资仍构成主要支撑，其中数
据中心等 AI 相关信息基础设施投资保持较强动能。劳动力市场边际降温，就业
增长和工资压力有所放缓，失业率小幅升至 4.2%，但尚未出现明显恶化。通胀
方面，能源及生产端成本压力有所增强，通胀风险仍未消退。美联储虽具备暂
缓加息、进一步观察政策传导的空间，但在通胀风险尚未充分消退的背景下，
维持限制性政策立场仍有必要。判断当前货币政策是否具有足够的限制性，关
键不仅在于政策利率本身，更在于其相对于自然利率的水平。美联储近期工作
论文指出，持续的财政赤字和政府债务积累可能推升长期自然利率，进而抬高
货币政策的中性基准，使相同水平的政策利率较过去具有更弱的实际限制性。
近年来美国财政赤字和公共债务长期处于高位，这一结构性变化意味着美联储
可能需要维持更高的实际政策利率，才能实现与过去相近的需求抑制效果。展
望未来，政策路径仍取决于经济韧性、通胀黏性与需求降温之间的相对变化。
若经济活动继续保持韧性、失业率维持在当前水平附近，同时核心通胀未能持
续回落，美联储仍存在进一步加息的理由。反之，若就业、消费和资本开支出
现持续走弱，带动通胀回落，美联储继续加息的必要性也将相应下降。 
就业降温打开暂停窗口，通胀仍是主要约束。北京时间 10 月 29 日凌晨，美联
储议息会议宣布保持联邦基金利率目标区间不变在 3.75%—4.00%。近期美国经
济呈现增长韧性延续、就业边际降温与通胀压力回升并存的格局。 经济增长方
面， 2026 年二季度美国实际 GDP 环比折年增速由此前公布的 1.5%上修至
2.2%，一季度增速亦由 2.1%上修至 2.5%。反映私人部门内生需求的实际私人国
内最终销售二季度环比折年增长 4.6%，较一季度明显加快。消费和私人投资仍
构成主要支撑，其中数据中心等 AI 相关信息基础设施投资保持较强动能。劳动
力市场方面，9 月美国新增非农就业仅 2.9 万人，7-8 月合计下修 6 万人，企业
新增用工需求较年中明显降温。失业率小幅升至 4.2%，自 3 月以来始终处于
4.1%—4.3%的窄幅区间。劳动参与率升至 61.8%，就业人口率亦小幅升至
59.2%。工资压力继续缓和， 9 月平均时薪环比仅上涨 0.1%，同比增速降至
3.0%。综合来看，劳动力市场有所降温，但尚未出现明显恶化的迹象。 通胀方
面，9 月 CPI 环比上涨 0.6%，同比升至 3.7%，其中汽油价格上涨 9.1%，贡献总
体 CPI 月度涨幅的一半以上，能源价格整体上涨 5.2%。核心 CPI 环比上涨
0.3%、同比上涨 2.6%，显示基础价格压力仍未完全消退。生产者价格压力更为
突出，9 月 PPI 环比上涨 1.3%、同比上涨 6.3%，最终需求商品价格环比上涨
2.6%；剔除食品、能源和贸易服务后仍环比上涨 0.5%、同比上涨 5.0%。美联储
最新褐皮书显示，多地企业继续报告能源、关税及其他投入成本上升，部分企
业通过提价或燃油附加费向下游转嫁成本。因此，在就业市场尚未明显失速的
情况下，美联储仍有必要维持限制性政策立场。本次保持利率不变并不意味着
通胀风险已经消退。未来是否需要进一步加息，则主要取决于能源及价格压力
是否进一步向核心通胀扩散，以及就业降温是否演变为更广泛的需求走弱。 
自然利率中枢上移可能削弱既定政策利率的实际约束。 判断货币政策是否具有
足够的限制性，关键在于判断实际政策利率相对于自然利率 /长期中性实际利率
的距离。自然利率通常指在通胀稳定、经济运行于潜在水平附近时，使储蓄与
投资实现均衡的实际利率水平。当自然利率上升时，即使政策利率保持不变，
其对总需求所形成的约束也会相应减弱。美联储近期工作论文从自然利率角度

### Page 2

宏观经济深度研究 - 2026 年 10 月 29 日 2 
证券研究 
研究了政府债务扩张的长期影响：论文利用 2021 年佐治亚州参议院决选构造自
然实验：在总统和众议院选举结果已经基本被金融市场消化的情况下，两席参
议院选举主要决定新政府能否通过预算协调程序实施额外财政刺激，因此选举
结果形成了一个相对独立于同期货币政策和经济周期变化的预期债务冲击。研
究估算，该事件使市场预期的额外财政刺激增加约 4500 亿美元、相当于当时
GDP 的约 2.1%，并进一步利用期限结构模型将实际国债收益率变化分解为长期
自然利率和实际期限溢价。结果显示，预期政府债务占 GDP 比重每提高 1 个百
分点，长期实际国债收益率约上升 3—4 个基点，其中长期自然利率上升约 1—2
个基点，实际期限溢价上升约 2—3 个基点。研究进一步利用 1985—2025 年的
中期债务预测进行时间序列检验，发现未来债务率每提高 1 个百分点，长期自
然利率约提高 1.24 个基点，说明政府债务增加对均衡实际利率存在统计上显著
且相对稳定的影响。尽管债务扩张对长期利率的影响更多体现为期限溢价上
升，但其对自然利率的影响仍具有统计显著性。这意味着持续的财政赤字和债
务积累可能逐步抬高货币政策的中性基准，使相同水平的政策利率具有较过去
更弱的实际限制性。 
未来政策路径仍然偏紧。 近年来美国财政赤字和公共债务持续处于较高水平，
使自然利率变化的政策意义进一步上升。美国国会预算办公室预计， 2026 财年
联邦财政赤字约为 1.9 万亿美元、相当于 GDP 的 5.8%，公众持有的联邦债务约
为 GDP 的 101%，并预计在 2036 年进一步升至 120%左右。持续较高的政府融
资需求意味着，财政因素可能逐步成为影响长期均衡实际利率的重要结构性力
量。若长期自然利率较疫情前有所抬升，相同水平的政策利率相对于自然利率
的正向缺口将有所收窄，对总需求形成的抑制作用也会相应减弱。因此，从自
然利率视角看，本次暂停加息并不意味着当前利率水平已经形成充分的限制
性，更多反映 9 月加息后的政策传导仍需观察，就业增长有所放缓也提高了连
续加息的门槛。考虑到持续财政赤字和政府债务积累不仅可能通过短期需求渠
道增加通胀压力，也可能通过改变长期储蓄与资金需求的均衡关系，对中性实
际利率形成上行压力。这意味着，在其他条件相同的情况下，美联储可能需要
维持更高的实际政策利率，才能形成与过去相近的需求抑制效果。展望未来，
如果经济活动继续保持韧性、失业率稳定在当前水平附近，同时核心通胀未能
持续回落，则美联储仍有进一步提高政策利率、扩大实际政策利率相对于自然
利率正向缺口的理由。参考 9 月 SEP，2026 年和 2027 年末联邦基金利率预测中
位数为 4.1%，这一路径仍与再加息 25 个基点大体一致。相反，如果未来就业、
消费和资本开支出现持续走弱，通胀同时回落，则实际政策利率还会在名义利
率不变的情况下被动上升，从而进一步增强货币政策的限制性，美联储继续加
息的必要性也将相应下降。

参考文献： 
Bhatt, A., Diercks, A. M., Eyal, B., & Skaperdas, A. (2026). The Causal Effect of Debt 
on Interest Rates . Finance and Economics Discussion Series 2026-031, Board of 
Governors of the Federal Reserve System.

### Page 3

重要披露 
工银国际的股票评级 
超越基准 ：预期未来 12 个月内股票总回报（包括股息）超越相应市场基准指数 20%以上 
中性 ：预期未来 12 个月内股票总回报（包括股息）对比相应市场基准指数在-20%至 20%以内 
落后基准 ：预期未来 12 个月内股票总回报（包括股息）落后相应市场基准指数 20%以上

代產品參考設計， 營收規模放大的同時體現獲利率效益， 獲利金額擴大以及營運資
金運用，並樹立組裝產業進入門檻。研究部參考近期公告之財務及產業資訊，預期
鴻海 2H26~2027 年營運具上修空間 ， 調整後預估鴻海 2026 年/2027 年稅後 EPS 
18.79 元/25.46 元。惟近期總體利率環境影響股價評價，目標本益比從 15 倍調整
至 14 倍，維持鴻海買進建議，目標價 356 元(14x2027FEPS)。

鴻海科技日展現垂直整合能力  統合零組件到機櫃生產：(1) 鴻海科技日強調展現
垂直整合能力及精密加工能力，提供客戶 one stop shop，垂直整合、設計製造服
務。鴻海具備伺服器 Level 1 到 Level 12 的生產能力，從螺絲、零組件、機構件
等至伺服器系統整機櫃。鴻海團隊從產品設計開始即整合組裝、良率、服務零組件
到整系統最佳化的解決方案。(2) 鴻海協助輝達、Google 打造 AI 工廠、AI 基礎
建設，以自動化縮短資料中心建造時程。(3) 施耐德電機、東元提供電力設備，與
鴻海進一步合作打造模組化資料中心。 4. 本田與三菱電機展示與鴻海在電動車未
來自動駕駛、駕駛監控系統、 ECU 等合作方向。 (5) 鴻海同時推出新電動車平
台,Model A+ 、K 系列。A+為休旅車，其特色為取消 B 柱讓車內寬敞，並將配置 
L4 自動駕駛感測器。Model K 為輕型卡車，提供短程 120 公里、長程 250 公里車
型選擇。 
展場上鴻海集團展出 AI 伺服器相關產品，1. 與 Vertiv 合作液冷基建的 AI Data 
Hall 解決方案。(2) 展出 NVIDIA DSX POD 解決方案，以及 Vera Rubin 世代 
Compute Tray、Switch Tray、CPO Switch、CPU Tray、LPU Tray 等相關產品。

EPS 上修至 5.85 元。

### Page 1

M
Update
King Yuan Electronics Co Ltd  | Asia Pacific
2027 outlook remains
promising; OW
4Q26 guidance cut from the major product delay and weaker smartphone
demand: The company cut 4Q26 revenue guidance  from up >10% to high-single
digit QoQ growth, given the R-series GPU shipment delays and weaker smartphone/
consumer demand. The gross margin should also decline slightly to 36-37%, given
the capacity build but lower-than-expected utilization rate.

1Q27 and 2027 demand remains robust: However, the company reiterated that
delayed demand will be pushed out to 1Q27, suggesting 1Q27 revenue will be up at
least 10-15% QoQ and gross margin will recover to >40%. Looking ahead into 2027,
demand remains robust, and the company expects 2027 revenue to grow 40-50%
YoY with margins >40%.
KYEC in-house burn-in oven could start to support MediaTek TPU: Despite  the
shipment delay from Micro Control Company (MCC), MediaTek management still

raised its 4Q26 TPU revenue projection for both 4Q26 and 2027. We believe it
relates to KYEC's supports of its in-house burn-in oven by end-2026, which could
potentially leave more share to KYEC against ASE if MCC's oven shipments continue
to fall short in  2027.

Key Takeaways
3Q26 results largely in line with both MSe and consensus, but 4Q26 both
revenue and margin guidance missed.

Despite the near-term headwind from the supply chain leading to KYEC's weaker
performance, we expect GPU, TPU, and CPU demand to all ramp up rapidly from
1Q27.
KYEC US capacity is on track for mass production by 4Q27 and full-year
contribution in 2028.
We stay OW on robust 2027 demand, margin recovery, and new capacity
expansion with strong customers' commitment.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

Analyst Industry Views

below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

### Page 3

Vanguard International Semi. (5347.TWO)
09 November 2026 Citi Research
3
Pricing outlook in the upward trend
Management remains constructive on the pricing environment as mature-node
supply continues to tighten. VIS emphasized its preference for negotiated price
adjustments rather than aggressively exploiting supply shortages, aiming to
maintain sustainable customer relationships. Importantly, more LTAs will expire in
2027 than in 2026, creating additional opportunities for contract repricing. In our

view, the supply-demand environment remains favorable for further pricing
improvements, although the magnitude and timing remain uncertain.
AI Revenue Contribution to Exceed 10% in 2026, With
Further Expansion in 2027
AI-related demand is becoming an increasingly important growth driver for VIS,
particularly across PMICs and discrete power devices used in data centers.
Management estimated that AI-related applications contributed a high-single-
digit percentage of revenue in 2025, rising to slightly above 10% in 2026, with

further increases expected in 2027. The company anticipates additional market
share gains in both PMICs and discrete devices as integrated device manufacturers
increasingly outsource production to foundries. The Singapore 12-inch fab will
further expand Vanguard’s exposure to AI-related technologies, including
interposers and advanced power management applications. We believe its AI
related revenue could reach 20-30% in 2027 and that should support a more

favorable product mix and reduce dependence on consumer electronics.
Given more favorable product mix and ASP trend, as well as interposer revenue
contribution, we lift our 2027 /2028 EPS by 7%/5%. We maintain our Buy rating
with lower TP at NT$160, based on lower PER of 20x of our 2027 EPS estimate
(lowered from 25x), reflecting potential policy and macro uncertainties.

Vanguard International Semi. (5347.TWO)
09 November 2026 Citi Research
7
Vanguard International Semi.
Company description
Vanguard International Semiconductor Corporation (VIS) is a specialty IC
foundry service provider, with existing capacity mainly focused on 8-inch. VIS
offers a wide range of process technologies, including high voltage, ultra-high
voltage, and BCD. In 2024, VIS and NXP Semiconductors established the
VSMC joint venture in Singapore, with its first 12-inch fab set to begin initial

production in 2027.
Investment strategy
We rate Vanguard a Buy as we expect it to benefit from robust PMIC sales
momentum, driven by strong AI demand and improving industrial demand.
VIS is gaining market share in PMIC as clients are looking for non-Chinese
suppliers. Moreover,  we believe VIS should benefit from transfer orders from
TSMC on the 8-inch wafer process. We think VIS has already passed its
trough and expect a gradual recovery into 2027.

Valuation
Our target price for VIS of NT$160 is based on 20x 2027E P/E, which we see
as justified by its robust PMIC sales growth momentum and increasing
outflow order opportunity from TSMC with support from its consistent cash
dividend policy. Our target price equates to 2026/27E P/B of 3.8x/3.7x.
Risks
Key downside risks to our target price include: 1) weaker 8-inch demand along
with higher costs due to inflation or more severe ASP competition from

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

## Document 200: 261108_gs_cotech

Source PDF: all report pdf/261108_gs_cotech.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-08
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Key Data  _____________________________________

GS Forecast  __________________________________

GS Factor Profile  ______________________________

Co-Tech’s 3Q26 core business (operating income) was 7/5% lower 
than GSe/BBG consensus, mainly due to higher summer electricity 
rates which led to a 0.7ppt QoQ decrease in 3Q26 GPM, and came in 
1.4/1.5ppt below GSe/BBG consensus. Product mix continues to 
improve with HVLP3+ copper contribution at close to 20% (vs. 
15-20% in 2Q26). Co-Tech’s OPEX increased to NT$90mn (vs. 
NT$83mn in 2Q26). NI was 8/4% lower than GSe/BBG consensus 
mainly due to the lower than expected GM.  
For 4Q26, we expect Co-Tech’s revenue will increase by 8% QoQ, 
mainly driven by (1) price hikes of ~10% QoQ across all copper foil 
products (we saw price hike starting to accelerate from late June, 
and we expect to see prices increase to further accelerate in 4Q26), 
(2) initial shipment of higher ASP/margin next gen products, 
including HVLP5 and RG313+, and (3) higher HVLP3+ copper foil 
contribution due to an increase in capacity (HVLP3+ capacity could 
reach 250 tons/month by end of 2026 vs. 200-250 tons/month in 
3Q26). On GM, we expect the 4Q26 level to be 31.4% (4.7ppt QoQ 
increase), mainly due to the improved product mix while the 
summer electricity rates impact comes to an end (the impact ended 
by mid-October). 
CB issuance plan announced to fund further capacity expansion 
in Malaysia: Co-Tech announced on Nov 6 its plan to issue CB 
(Convertible Bond) of up to NT$7bn, supporting the company’s 
Plant 4 expansion in Malaysia, which the company is expected to 
spend NT$7-10bn on two phases, with each phase expected to add 
~800tons/month of effective HVLP4+ capacity (based on yield of 
50-60%). The two phases are targeted to reach MP by end of 
2028/29, respectively. Full conversion of the CB would result in less 
than 10% EPS dilution per GSe (based on the average share price 
over the past week), while each phase of the expansion could 
contribute NT$11bn+ in revenue at full operation based on our 
estimate (vs. Co-Tech’s 2025 revenue of NT$7.9bn).

### Page 3

Investment view - Capacity expansion extended into 2029, still insufficient to meet 
with client demand 
We maintain our positive view on Co-Tech’s revenue and profitability outlook, 
underpinned by (1) a substantial capacity ramp-up, with Co-Tech’s high-end capacity 
will increase by 3.5x+/75%+/50%+ by 2027/28/29 end (vs. 2026 end), outpacing the 
expansion expected from other major competitors and supporting further market share 
gains, and (2) supply condition for high-end copper foil remains tight, supporting a 
better pricing environment, and we expect price increase to accelerate in coming 
quarters/years. 
We continue to believe Co-Tech is well-positioned to benefit from the structural demand 
for high end copper foil in a shortage environment, as the company has announced 
capacity expansion plans for a new Plant 4 in Malaysia (see here), with capacity already 
secured by multiple customers. The company now targets its monthly HVLP3+ effective 
capacity to reach 833/1666/2500 tons/month by 2027/28/29 end (vs. current HVLP3+ 
effective capacity of 200-250 tons/month). Despite the proactive capacity expansion, 
the planned capacity additions remain insufficient to fully meet with demand from key 
customers. This should enable the company to further expand market share amid high 
end copper foil supply shortage environment, and we expect the company’s HVLP3+ 
market share to further expand to 41/53% in 2027/28E (vs. 19% in 2026E).  
We expect high-end copper foil TAM to reach US$2.7bn by 2028E (Exhibit 1, with 133% 
2025-28E CAGR), following our update on global AI PCB/CCL TAM (see here). We now 
believe the shortage ratio for HVLP3+ industry to be 39-48% in 2026-28E (Exhibit 4, 
slightly lower than our previous estimate, which was 45-55% in 2026-28E, as we factor 
in the latest capacity expansion plans), with the shortage already starting in 2H26, as the 
ramp-up of mainstream AI server projects increasingly adopts HVLP4+ copper foil. The 
wide shortage ratio would suggest an elevated industry UTR and provide further support 
for price increase over the coming quarters/years.  
We maintain Buy on Co-Tech, with a new TP of NT$990 from NT$940.

demand (133% 2025-28E CAGR), which could account for 
38% of the global PCB CF TAM in 2028

Exhibit 2: High-end CCL shipment should continue to 
increase, with solid demand from AI server/Switch 
high-end copper foil demand (tons/year)

higher copper cost, which will lead to slower GM expansion.

22x 2028E EPS, which is +1SD above the company’s upcycle valuation in the past

Investment Thesis & PT methodology and risks

Investment Thesis - Co-Tech 
Co-Tech is a global leading general server copper foil supplier, with ~60% market share 
in 2025 per GSe. We expect the company to enjoy solid share gain opportunities in the 
near term, considering its proactive HVLP capacity expansion plan and strong 
know-how in the advanced copper foil industry. Also, adoption of HVLP4 copper foil 
from AI servers and 1.6T networking switches starting in 2H26 should be a key demand 
driver for Co-Tech, in our view. Additionally, the company’s proactive pricing strategy 
and ability to improve production yield should drive revenue momentum in the long 
term.  
We are Buy rated as we believe Co-Tech will see better revenue/profitability in the long 
term given its shift to a more premium product mix along with proactive capacity plans 
(Plant 3 to be ready by end of 2027 and Plant 4 phase 1 & 2 to be ready by end of 
2028/29) and this should lead to strengthened valuations.

Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$990 is based on a 22x 2028E P/E, which is 
+1SD above the company’s average upcycle valuation in the past 10-years. 
Key downside risks: (1) earlier than expected CPO adoption in the computing IC, (2) 
slower than expected yield rate improvement speed, and (3) much stronger than 
expected capacity growth from HVLP peers, limiting pricing power

and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

### Page 1

M
Idea
Muted momentum in near term: Chenbro's 3Q26 earnings fell short of market
expectations by ~30% on lower revenue and margins, owing to customer inventory
digestion, a longer qualification process for its ASIC customer's new product and a
less favorable revenue mix. Looking to 4Q26,  management guided for revenue to
stay roughly flat QoQ (Oct at NT$2,308mn, -7% MoM/+16% YoY) and GM to be
similar to that in 3Q26, as the overhang of new product qualification extends into

4Q26.
2027 still seen as a good year: We believe it continue to grow along with the new
AI server platforms, its customer's share gains and incremental contribution from
new products. We now model for revenue to grow by 35% YoY, GM to be roughly
flat at 29.3% and EPS to rise 39% YoY for 2027.
More diversification into the AI clusters: Chenbro has been leveraging its existing
expertise in mechanical components and customer relationship to diversify from

chassis into other mechanical components in AI applications. It started to see more
meaningful contribution from racks at 5-10% in 2025, with ~10% likely in 2026 and
potentially topping 20% in 2027. For the longer term, it could step into other
mechanical components in the liquid cooling space. Although these efforts could
open up more opportunities for Chenbro to grow, they don't all carry higher-than-
average margins, so potential dilution could be a factor to monitor.

Global footprint continues to expand: To improve its supply chain resilience and
local production capability, Chenbro has several capacity expansion plans globally,
with  Taiwan remaining  the hub for high-end production and R&D. In the pipeline,
Malaysia will likely see new capacity ramping from 4Q26, Vietnam from 2H27 and
US pilot run/MP in 4Q27/2H28. Capex was guided at NT$2.5-3.0bn for 2026.
Downgrade to EW: We believe Chenbro should remain one of the competitive

players in the AI mechanical component space, but near-term hiccups might mute
business momentum. Despite valuation approaching its trough, we see no strong
catalyst in the coming quarters. We thus reduce our rating to EW and lower our PT
by 61% to NT$630 (12x 2027e P/E, implying a PEG of 0.3 vs. the average of 0.5 in
2020-25).
What’s Changed
Chenbro (8210.TW) From To

in the near term, and thus downgrade the stock.

Results Summary
Off revenue of NT$7,395mn (-5% QoQ/+31% YoY), GM contracted by 6.8ppt QoQ to
25.8%, mainly due to a less-favorable product mix and costs associated with new product
qualification. This was short of MSe at 31.9% and consensus at 30.2%, or by 6.1ppt and
4.3ppt, respectively.
Opex was NT$546mn (opex ratio of 7.4%) vs. MSe of NT$686mn (opex ratio 8.2%) and
consensus of NT$617mn (opex ratio 7.5%), leading to operating profit of NT$1,373mn

(OPM 18.6%). This was 31% lower than MSe at NT$1,996mn (OPM 23.9%) and 26% lower
than consensus at NT$1,854mn (OPM 22.6%).
With a non-op gain of NT$2mn (FX gain of NT$18mn), net profit of NT$1,005mn (EPS NT
$8.21) was 33% lower than MSe at NT$1,500mn (EPS NT$12.25) and 29% below
consensus at NT$1,412mn (EPS NT$11.25).

Idea
6
Valuation Methodology
Our price target of NT$630 (previously NT$1,600) is derived from 12x 2027e P/E (vs.
27x 2027e previously): We use price/earnings as our valuation methodology because we
believe it appropriately captures Chenbro's earnings growth potential. We lower our
target multiple to factor in weaker market sentiment toward the stock on softer
fundamental performance and overall to AI names, as well as muted near-term business
momentum. We believe our target P/E multiple of 12x is justified by an earnings CAGR of

32% seen for 2025-28e, implying a PEG of 0.4. This is lower than the PEG of 0.8 in 2014-
24, when the stock traded at an average P/E of 12x for a 15% earnings CAGR.
Bull case scenario value - NT$1,600 (-29%): We apply a 30x P/E multiple (38x 2027e
previously) to our base case estimate for 2027. This scenario mainly assumes a better
outlook for AI capex and more project wins from  new customers.
Bear case scenario value - NT$380 (-42%): We apply a 7x P/E multiple (11x 2027e

previously) to our base case estimate for 2027. This scenario mainly assumes lukewarm
datacenter demand amid softening IT capex budgets and slower progress in its new
product offerings.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

## Document 225: 35岁不会写代码？这波 AI 红利，可能就轮到你了

Source PDF: all report pdf/35岁不会写代码？这波 AI 红利，可能就轮到你了.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

最缺的不是更强的  AI 模型，⽽是既懂业务、⼜能把  AI 真正⽤起来的⼈。
摘要：
今年以来，全球科技公司已宣布裁员近  20 万⼈。与此同时，有⼀个  AI 岗位的招聘需求却在爆
发：美国的招聘⼴告三年涨了  30 多倍，薪资中位数约  19.5 万美元；国内相关职位的发布量⼀
年涨了  21 倍。
OpenAI 、 Anthropic 、微软、亚⻢逊、⾕歌，⼏家巨头前后砸下近百亿美元，有的开新公司，有
的建新部⻔，抢的都是这类⼈才。最反常识的是：这个岗位最稀缺的本事，恰恰不是写代码。
Anthropic ：培养  1 万个 “ 把  AI 送进企业的⼈ ”
10 ⽉  2 ⽇， Claude 的开发商  Anthropic 宣布投⼊  1 亿美元成⽴ “Claude 前沿学院 ” （ Claude
Frontier Academy ），⽬标是在  2027 年底前培养  1 万名 “ 前沿部署⼯程师 ” 。
⾸批学员是从合作伙伴中选出的在职⼯程师，来⾃埃森哲、摩根⼠丹利、诺和诺德和⼏家顶级
咨询公司。培训分两段：先是  4 天线下集训，从⼀次 “ 模拟企业部署 ” 开始；考核通过后，学员回
到⾃⼰公司，带⼀个真实项⽬做  12 周实战。⾸批认证预计在  2027 年初完成。
在  Anthropic 看来，企业落地  AI 的瓶颈不在模型，⽽在于缺少能推动变⾰的⼈。负责这个项⽬
的⾼管  Steve Corfield 说，客⼾现在缺的，是既懂业务怎么运转、⼜能亲⼿把  AI 从⼀个想法做
成上线系统的⼈。
这只是今年  AI 巨头抢⼈⼤战中的最新⼀例。

砸下百亿美元，巨头们都在抢同⼀种⼈才
今年  5 ⽉， OpenAI 成⽴了专⻔帮企业落地  AI 的 “OpenAI 部署公司 ” ，由  TPG 领投，⾸轮融资
超过  40 亿美元。同⽉， Anthropic 与⿊⽯、 Hellman & Friedman 、⾼盛合资成⽴了⼀家  AI 服
务公司，各⽅初始投资约  15 亿美元。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

6 ⽉底到  7 ⽉初，亚⻢逊  AWS 和微软先后出⼿： AWS 投⼊  10 亿美元，组建⾃⼰的部署⼯程
团队；微软投⼊  25 亿美元成⽴新部⻔，把约  6000 名⼯程专家派驻到客⼾企业。 9 ⽉，⾕歌云
和埃森哲成⽴联合业务部⻔，计划培训最多  1000 名埃森哲⼯程师，专⻔派驻客⼾现场。
过去这些公司⽐的是谁的模型更强、参数更⼤、跑分更⾼，如今却不约⽽同地把钱砸向同⼀个
⽅向：派⼈进客⼾公司，把  AI 真正⽤起来。
这类⼈才在业内有个统⼀的叫法：FDE （ Forward Deployed Engineer ），中⽂常译作“ 前沿部
署⼯程师 ”。
招聘量三年猛涨 30 倍，⼤⼚开出百万年包
熟悉美股的读者，对这个词的源头不会陌⽣。⼗多年前， Palantir （帕兰泰尔）就系统化地推⾏
了这种做法：⼯程师不坐在总部写通⽤产品，⽽是常驻客⼾现场，和客⼾⽤同⼀套数据、扛同
⼀个业务指标，把模糊的需求、分散的数据⼀点点变成能⽤的系统。 Palantir CEO Alex Karp 有

⼀个被业内反复引⽤的判断：⼤模型当然重要，但⾄少在未来⼏年，价值在于落地实施。
如今，这套做法正在变成⼀个热⻔职业。据劳动⼒市场分析机构  Lightcast 统计， 2023 年全美
FDE 招聘⼴告不到  200 条， 2026 年前九个⽉已超过  7300 条，三年涨了  30 多倍。⼴告标出的
薪资中位数约  19.5 万美元，是全美招聘⼴告薪资中位数的三倍多。发布  FDE ⼴告最多的雇主
中，德勤、埃森哲等咨询公司排在⾕歌等科技巨头之前。可⻅  FDE 早已不只是  AI 公司的技术
岗，凡是要把  AI ⽤进业务的地⽅，都需要这样的⼈。
FDE 这股⻛已经刮到国内。脉脉平台数据显⽰， 2026 年上半年，平台上  FDE 相关职位的发布
量同⽐飙升  21 倍。字节跳动的 “ ⾖包  AI ⼤模型  FDE” 年包最⾼约  105 万元，蚂蚁数科、智谱等
也开出了  90 万元以上的年包。但即便开出⾼薪，合适的⼈依然难招：猎聘⼤数据研究院统计，
2025 年  9 ⽉⾄  2026 年  8 ⽉，国内  FDE ⼈才的供需⽐只有  0.28 ，相当于将近  4 个岗位抢  1 个

⼈。
这个缺⼝还在被政策放⼤。⼯信部  8 ⽉底印发通知，⿎励  AI 应⽤服务商搭建  FDE 团队、扎根
⽤⼾现场；武汉光⾕提出  3 年培育  1000 名  FDE ，上海提出要建⽴超千⼈规模的  FDE ⼯程师储
备库。企业、服务商、地⽅政府都在要⼈，合格的  FDE 远远不够⽤。
AI 落地难在业务，最缺的恰恰是懂业务的⼈
看到这⾥，很多⼈会想：这是年轻程序员的机会，我不写代码，也过了转⾏的年纪。但不妨看
看  Anthropic 怎么描述⼀个好的  FDE ：熟悉  Agent 系统、擅⻓解决问题、善于沟通、有⼯程经
验。这四条⾥，有⼀半在技术之外。
AI 落地最难的部分，本来就不在代码。过去两年，⼏乎每家企业都买过  AI 产品、做过试点，真
正跑进⽇常业务的却少得多。标普全球的调查显⽰，放弃⼤部分  AI 项⽬的企业，⼀年内从  17%
涨到了  42% ； IDC 和联想  2025 年的报告更直接： 88% 的  AI 概念验证项⽬，最后都没能投⼊
⽣产。
演⽰时⼀切顺利，⼀进真实业务，就撞上脏数据、⽼系统、权限审批、员⼯习惯，还有那个绕

不开的问题：出了错谁负责。这也是  FDE 和驻场外包的区别：外包是甲⽅说什么就做什么；
FDE 要先判断这件事该不该⽤  AI 、⽤哪个模型、花多少钱、怎么证明有效，再动⼿上线。
所以  FDE 真正值钱的那⼀半能⼒，是懂业务。⼀份合同哪⼏条要紧，⼀张报表哪个数字会出
事，⼀个审批流程卡在哪⼀步，哪件事交给机器会让⽼板睡不着觉，这些  AI 替代不了，在⾏业
⾥⼲了⼗⼏、⼆⼗年的⼈反⽽最清楚。

### Page 3

腾讯云副总裁张渝前不久的⼀句话，戳中了很多企业的痛处：过去花⼤价钱请外部团队做项
⽬， " 项⽬做完，团队撤场，能⼒也跟着带⾛了 " 。越来越多企业意识到，与其等外部团队来了⼜
⾛，不如让⾃⼰懂业务的⼈⻓出这项能⼒。
如果你已经在⾏业⾥深耕多年，那么你缺的从来不是经验，⽽是把经验交给  AI 去执⾏的那⼀
步：知道今天的  AI 能做什么、做不了什么，该怎么搭、要花多少钱、出了错怎么兜底。我们这
次的训练营，就是为这⼀步准备的。
两天⾛完  AI 落地最后⼀公⾥
华尔街⻅闻⾸期「 FDE 实战训练营」将于  11 ⽉  14 ⽇⾄  15 ⽇在上海开班。这是⼀个线下实战
营，限量  50 席，在两天时间⾥，带你亲⼿搭出⾃⼰的第⼀个  AI Agent ，⾛完⼀遍从选模型、算
成本到评测上线的全流程。
先说说  Agent （智能体）是什么。很多⼈对  AI 的印象还停留在 “ 问⼀句答⼀句 ” 的聊天上，但
Agent 不⼀样：你交给它⼀件事，它会⾃⼰查资料、打开软件、读写⽂件、交出结果，做错了还
能⾃⼰返⼯。巨头们派  FDE 进企业，部署的正是这种技术。
两天训练营共  12 个模块，从模型怎么选、价格怎么算，⼀直讲到评测、成本优化和安全上线，
都是⼤⼚把  AI 做上线时真正在⽤的⽅法。主讲讲师  Right 曾任腾讯⾼级算法⼯程师，主导过微
信核⼼  AI Agent 项⽬的算法设计和规模化落地，在  Agent 系统设计、模型训练、 Harness 架构
⽅向有深厚积累。他还在  NeurIPS 、 ACL 等顶级会议发表过多篇  Agent ⽅向论⽂，相关⼯作被
Google DeepMind 引⽤。
课程适合谁
企业主和管理者：想弄清  AI 到底能替公司做哪些事、每件事花多少钱、⻛险在哪⾥
⾦融、法务、财务、运营、咨询等专业⼈⼠：⼿上有⼤量重复⼯作，想亲⼿做⼀个⼯具交给
AI
想在  AI 时代给⾃⼰多留⼀项硬技能的⼈

三⼗年前，先学会⽤  Excel 的那批⼈，在后来的⼆⼗年⾥少熬了⽆数个夜。
今天巨头们花百亿美元在抢懂业务、⼜会⽤  AI 的⼈，⽽你已经有了⼀半。剩下那⼀半，就在这
两天⾥补⻬。
点击海报，锁定早⻦席位

營 運 分 析

富世達為 AI基礎設施與高階摺疊機轉軸的關鍵供應商 
富世達股份有限公司成立於民國 2001 年，為奇鋐(3017)持股 16.87%的轉投
資公司，總部與研發中心位於新北新莊，並於越南、中國深圳設有生產基地，
提供各類金屬沖壓設計與製造，主要產品為伺服器組件(滑軌、水冷快接頭)，
與摺疊手機、筆記型電腦、平板電腦之轉軸零件，從消費性電子零組件廠轉
型為 AI基礎設施與高階摺疊機轉軸的關鍵供應商，主要出貨終端包含華為、
Motorola 等摺疊機，與各大AI伺服器平台(NVIDIA、AMD、ASIC等)機櫃用
的滑軌與水冷快接頭。

26Q3 伺服器產品營收占比上升至 81.3%，帶動毛利率大幅優於預期。 
富世達 26Q3 營收 45.0 億元(QoQ+20.4%/YoY+26.2%，優於兆豐前次預期
4%)，因 VR200、ASIC 平台小量拉貨與非奇鋐之快接頭客戶規模上升使伺
服器快接頭營收增長強勁；毛利率 38.0%(季增 3.9ppts/年增 10.3ppts，優於
兆豐預期 2.9ppts)，主因產品組合改善，伺服器產品營收占比超越 80%；營
業淨利 14.6 億元 (QoQ+39.9%/YoY+84.2%)， 稅 後 淨 利12.4 億元
(QoQ+46.3%/YoY+80.8%)，EPS 18.1元(前次預估 15.9 元)。

資料來源：Cmoney、兆豐投顧整理與預估

QD 受惠 GPU與 ASIC雙平台放量及規格升級，為伺服器產品主要成長動能 
26Q3 富世達伺服器產品占營收 81.3%，其中快接頭(QD)約 90%，整體單季
稼動率約 80~90%；而目前新產能開出後 QD 產能已達 1350 萬顆/季。今年
QD 營收貢獻以 GPU 平台為主，明年 ASIC 比重將持續提高，預期 2028 年
ASIC 比重有望超越 GPU；其中(1)AWS Trn3 已配合客戶時程出貨，將於
27H1顯著貢獻營收。(2)Google TPU V9世代有望由現行的 NSP1接頭，變
為導入混用 QD+NSP1接頭，以優化未來光模塊、DIMM 等部件潛在導入水
冷系統後維護的容易性。(3)Meta MTIA 與微軟 MAIA 等 AISC 專案也將於
27H2 出貨帶動 QD 市場規模提升。而在 Rubin Ultra 世代，將導入 ZQD 於
運算托盤(取代部份的 UQD)與 rack manifold之快接頭，且ZQD單顆價值預
估將為現行 MQD與 UQD 單價的兩倍，ZQD 預計 12月進入量產準備。 
規格面，VR200 單櫃 QD 數量約 538 顆(含 rack manifold 88 顆)，Rubin 
Ultra(VR300)將為 574~664顆，預估 QD量價齊揚趨勢延續，為2027~2028
年主要成長動能。

AI 滑軌陸續通過驗證，27年可望隨 VR200與 ASIC 專案量產放量 
滑軌事業目前月產能維持 35 萬套，目前以通用型為主，稼動率約 3 成；隨
明年 AI專案的量產，稼動率可望提升至50~60%以上，毛利率也將隨著 ASIC
水冷專案的放量同步改善，水冷櫃滑軌對承重、緩衝與平行度要求較高，ASP
亦較高，公司預期明年滑軌維持高度成長，後續將視訂單規劃擴廠。

- 4 - 
個股報告

華為摺疊機新品拉貨遞延與記憶體推升價格，轉軸營收短期承壓 
華為新機種受記憶體影響拉貨延後，加上終端需求轉弱，26Q3富世達手機
軸承營收占比降至 15.5%，預期26Q4手機轉軸拉貨動能有限。而華為 26H1
中國摺疊機市占逾 7成，摺疊機累計出貨突破 2,000 萬支；惟記憶體漲價使
單機平均成本增加約 200 美元，9/1 起主力機型最高調漲 17%，後續產品將
朝輕薄化發展並導入新材料，每代價格將重新議價，下一代機種仍由富世達
主供。雖蘋果 iPhone Duo 上市帶動摺疊機話題，將使摺疊機長期接受度與
滲透率同步提高，但短期消費性電子仍受記憶體價格推升單價而影響終端購
買意願。

26Q4 營收下修、毛利率上修，獲利預估小幅調降 
預估富世達 26Q4 營收 54.0 億元(QoQ+20.0%/YoY+35.8%，較原估下修
8.2%，主因摺疊機軸承拉貨不如預期 )；毛利率 38.1%(季增 0.1ppts/年增
9.0ppts，較原估上修 1.1ppts，主因 QD 生產進入規模化，帶動高毛利伺服
器比重上升)；稅後淨利 14.8 億元(QoQ+19.4%/YoY+97.3%，較原估下修
4.7%)，EPS 21.63元(原估 22.7 元)。

Điểm nhấn thị trường 
Chỉ số, nhóm ngành, khối ngoại và phái sinh

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX tăng điểm trong tuần qua, +68.24 điểm tương ứng +4.00%, với thanh khoản cải 
thiện. Thiết bị điện, Khách sạn - Nhà hàng và Thiết bị dịch vụ y tế là các nhóm ngành có 
mức tăng rõ rệt nhất trong tuần. Khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần 
trước với giá trị -966 tỷ đồng, tập trung ở các mã FPT, ACB và VCB; mặt khác, tự doanh duy 
trì mua ròng ở mức 137 tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại đảo chiều mua ròng 1,335 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần 
qua nhưng đang duy trì vị thế bán lũy kế -3,049 hợp đồng. Tự doanh đảo chiều mua ròng 
1,815 hợp đồng nhưng đang duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -6,000 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam hồi phục trở lại sau bốn tuần giảm liên tiếp, khi VN-Index bật tăng mạnh 
từ vùng hỗ trợ quanh 1.700 điểm với thanh khoản cải thiện. Sau nhịp điều chỉnh kéo dài 
khiến mặt bằng định giá về vùng hấp dẫn, lực cầu bắt đáy đã nhập cuộc quyết liệt hơn, 
với phiên 21/10 tăng gần 47 điểm — một trong những phiên tăng mạnh nhất nhiều tháng 
gần đây. Điểm tích cực và đáng chú ý nhất là khối ngoại đã xuất hiện hai phiên đảo chiều 
mua ròng trở lại sau khoảng 20 phiên bán ròng liên tiếp, tập trung vào nhóm Bluechip 
như VIC, TCB, VHM, MSN — tín hiệu đầu tiên cho thấy áp lực rút vốn ngoại có thể đang dần 
hạ nhiệt. Thông tin tích cực tác động lên thị trường đến từ các chỉ đạo của NHNN và Chính 
phủ về việc hạ mặt bằng lãi suất để hỗ trợ tăng trưởng kinh tế. Bên cạnh đó, mùa báo cáo 
KQKD quý 3 khả quan đang diễn ra cũng là yếu tố hỗ trợ xu hướng thị trường chung.

19/10/2026

Nguồn: V_KBSource

Triển vọng kinh tế 4Q2026 
Giữ nhịp tăng trưởng, vững vàng về itch

Một số dự báo chính của KBSV về kinh tế Việt Nam trong năm 2026:

1) Tăng trưởng GDP năm 2026 được dự báo sẽ đạt 9.0%, với động lực chính 
đến từ định hướng đẩy mạnh giải ngân đầu tư công của Chính phủ, dòng 
vốn FDI duy trì tích cực và hoạt động sản xuất công nghiệp phục hồi theo 
xuất khẩu. Về rủi ro, chúng tôi cho rằng Việt Nam sẽ tiếp tục đối mặt với 
một số yếu tố bất định từ bên ngoài, bao gồm: rủi ro xung đột Mỹ – Iran 
kéo dài hơn dự kiến, xu hướng tăng của mặt bằng lãi suất toàn cầu gây gia 
tăng áp lực tài chính và kéo giảm tổng cầu, cùng nguy cơ vỡ bong bóng AI.

2) Chúng tôi nâng dự báo lạm phát bình quân năm 2026 của Việt Nam lên 
mức 4.7% YoY, cao hơn mục tiêu kiểm soát lạm phát của Chính phủ, dựa 
trên kịch bản dự báo thận trọng hơn về diễn biến xung đột địa chính trị và 
giá năng lượng thế giới trong ngắn hạn.

3) Mặt bằng lãi suất trong quý IV được dự báo sẽ ổn định quanh vùng hiện 
tại và không xuất hiện thêm nhịp tăng đột biến, nhờ dòng tiền cải thiện 
từ giải ngân đầu tư công và kỳ vọng có thêm các biện pháp hỗ trợ thanh 
khoản trung dài hạn từ phía Chính phủ và NHNN.

4) Tỷ giá USD/VND trong quý cuối năm có thể chịu một số sức ép do Fed dự 
báo còn thêm một lần tăng lãi suất nữa trong năm. Dù vậy, chúng tôi cho 
rằng tỷ giá USD/VND khó tăng mạnh mà chỉ quay lại mức đầu năm, dựa 
trên kỳ vọng chỉ số DXY không biến động mạnh, nguồn cung ngoại tệ cải 
thiện (FDI giải ngân, kiều hối, nâng hạng TTCK) và chênh lệch lãi suất 
VND–USD duy trì trạng thái dương.

Tham khảo nội dung chi tiết trong báo cáo tại đây.

### Page 4

21/10/2026

CTCP VÀNG BẠC ĐÁ QUÝ PHÚ NHUẬN (PNJ)  
Cập nhật ĐHĐCĐ bất thường 21/10/2026

Tái cơ cấu để xử lý áp lực thanh khoản  
— ĐHĐCĐ bất thường PNJ ngày 21/10/2026 đã thông qua điều chỉnh kế hoạch kinh 
doanh và phương án phát hành riêng lẻ để bổ sung vốn tái cấu trúc. Ban lãnh đạo đặt 
ra các vấn đề trọng yếu là đáp ứng nhu cầu thanh khoản và chuẩn bị nguồn lực để thực 
hiện các cam kết thu đổi đã tích lũy trong quá khứ. Bên cạnh đó, các cổ đông tham dự 
cũng đặt các câu hỏi liên quan đến tình hình hoạt động qua các điểm chính (1) dòng 
tiền hoạt động kinh doanh; (2) diễn biến thị trường sau khi PNJ áp dụng chính sách thu 
đổi mới; (3) hiệu quả hoạt động sau tái cấu trúc.

Nội dung chính tại đại hội 
— Theo Ban lãnh đạo, thông tin liên quan đến P-Lab từ đầu tháng 7 khiến khách hàng gia 
tăng bán lại sản phẩm. Tổng giá trị thu đổi và mua lại trong tháng đạt gần 6,000 tỷ VND, 
so với khoảng 7,000 tỷ VND trong cả năm 2025. Theo BCTC riêng, nguồn tiền khả dụng 
giảm từ khoảng 3,700 tỷ VND cuối Q2/2026 xuống 770 tỷ VND cuối Q3/2026, đã bao 
gồm khoản vay 700 tỷ VND từ Chủ tịch HĐQT. Tỷ trọng kim cương và trang sức kim 
cương trong tổng tồn kho tăng từ 41% lên 52%. 
— Q3/2026 ghi nhận lỗ sau thuế 258 tỷ VND trước phần tăng trích lập dự phòng. Để giảm 
áp lực thanh toán, PNJ kéo dài thời gian chi trả đối với kim cương lên 120 ngày từ tháng 
07/2026. Thay đổi chính sách cũng ảnh hưởng đến niềm tin khách hàng và sức mua, cụ 
thể doanh thu và lợi nhuận gộp Q3/2026 đạt lần lượt 6,995/609 tỷ VND, giảm 14%/62% 
YoY. Chi phí bán hàng và quản lý giảm 13% YoY chưa đủ bù đắp mức giảm lợi nhuận 
gộp, khiến PNJ lỗ sau thuế 258 tỷ VND, so với lãi 496 tỷ VND trong Q3/2025. 
— PNJ nâng giả định tỷ lệ thu đổi trên giá trị thu đổi còn lại lên 80%, so với 28.8% đối với 
nhóm sản phẩm giai đoạn 2020–2026 trong ước tính tại BCTC bán niên 2026. Tổng dự 
phòng tổn thất dự kiến tăng từ 2,267 tỷ VND lên 7,071 tỷ VND. Kế hoạch điều chỉnh ghi 
nhận doanh thu 39,057 tỷ VND, lỗ trước thuế 6,059 tỷ VND và lỗ sau thuế 6,271 tỷ VND. 
Theo Ban lãnh đạo, LNST loại trừ dự phòng ước khoảng 800 tỷ VND, chủ yếu nhờ kết 
quả Q1/2026. Khoản dự phòng phản ánh tổn thất ước tính cho toàn bộ kịch bản, không 
đồng nghĩa toàn bộ giá trị sẽ được chi trả ngay trong kỳ. 
— Giá trị cam kết thu đổi tích lũy đối với kim cương và trang sức kim cương tại cuối 
Q3/2026 được PNJ ước tính khoảng 21,000 tỷ VND. Với hàng đã bán, doanh nghiệp dự 
kiến chuyển đổi quyền lợi khách hàng sang sản phẩm và các chương trình ưu đãi. Với 
sản phẩm bán mới, chính sách thu đổi định hướng theo giá thị trường tại thời điểm 
giao dịch, thay cho tỷ lệ cố định trên giá trị hóa đơn, nhằm hạn chế tiếp tục tích lũy 
nghĩa vụ tiềm tàng trong tương lai theo cơ chế cũ. 
Tham khảo nội dung chi tiết trong báo cáo tại đây.

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX hình thành cây nến xanh thân dài trên khung tuần và đáng chú ý là cho phản ứng 
bật tăng ngay tại cạnh dưới của mẫu hình tam giác đi ngang trung hạn, cho thấy tín hiệu 
tích cực đã quay trở lại với xu hướng ngắn hạn. Trên khung ngày, sau 2 phiên điều chỉnh, 
rung lắc vào đầu tuần, chỉ số cũng đảo chiều tăng điểm khá dốc, với 3 cây nến xanh liên 
tiếp với thanh khoản được tăng cường, củng cố thêm tín hiệu xác nhận cho đà hồi phục. 
Sau nhịp bật tăng mạnh từ đáy, VNINDEX được dự báo sẽ sớm gặp phải áp lực điều chỉnh 
khi quay lên điểm giữa của dải đi ngang theo mẫu hình tam giác, tương ứng vùng kháng cự 
kế tiếp tại quanh 1810. Tuy nhiên, xu hướng ngắn hạn vẫn có phần nghiêng về chiều tăng, 
với tín hiệu xác nhận đáy của một số mã cổ phiếu sau quá trình sụt giảm kéo dài.

(Ghi chú: NĐT có thể dựa theo nhận định xu hướng thị trường để tham khảo khuyến nghị 
hành động tương ứng trong bảng phía trên) 
Kháng cự xa: 1850 (+-15) 
Kháng cự gần: 1810 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ gần: 1750 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ xa: 1710 (+-15)

trọng số được phân bổ đều giữa các mã.

Lưu ý:

giá khuyến nghị mua.

## Document 363: Cotality-Westpac NZ First Home Buyer Report, Oct 2026 (2026-10-01)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Cotality-Westpac NZ First Home Buyer Report, Oct 2026 (2026-10-01).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-10-01
Pages: 19
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

October 2026
First Home
Buyer Report

### Page 2

2 © 2026 CoreLogic NZ Limited t/as Cotality. Proprietary
Cotality’s
First Home Buyer
(FHB) Report has tracked
purchasing activity for
several years,
analysing
the
types of properties bought,
the prices paid, and broader
shifts in entry
-
level demand.
The latest edition updates
these measures and extends
the analysis to examine FHB
activity across different
price brackets.
Now in its fourth edition in
partnership with Westpac,
the report also draws on the

bank’s mortgage lending
data to provide additional
insight into borrower
characteristics. That
includes the average age of
FHB borrowers, a measure
not widely available
elsewhere, as well as loan
-
to
-
value ratios across new
mortgages.
For clarity, we define a first home
buyer purchase to be where no party
involved has owned property in New
Zealand before and are using a
mortgage to make the deal.
Note:
The Westpac FHB age and LVR data
are sourced from their lending records

and is not matched to the Cotality
database for this report.
Cotality – Westpac
First Home Buyer Report

### Page 3

3
© 2026 CoreLogic NZ Limited t/as Cotality. Proprietary
FHBs’ market share remains
at record high levels.
In Q3
2026, they accounted for
29.5% of property purchases
across NZ, surpassing the
previous mark of 28.3% only
set three months earlier. The
number of deals remains at
historically high levels too,
bucking the wider trend of
slowing sales volumes for
groups such as investors and
relocating owner
-
occupiers.
This strength for FHBs is
being seen right across the

country,
with market shares
generally running above their
own long
-
term averages
(since 2005). The wider
Wellington area remains a
hotspot for FHBs, accounting
for 37% of activity so far in
2026. But FHBs have also
been at least 30% of the
market in Hamilton and
Auckland, as well as Gisborne,
Rotorua, Hastings,
Palmerston North, and
Invercargill
–
South Waikato
and Waikato District too.
This high level of activity
hasn’t meant too many
compromises either;

standalone houses have
comprised 76% of all FHB
purchases so far in 2026, up
from 75% last year, and the
highest figure since 2020
(78%). To be fair, FHBs have
had to pay a little more, with a
median price so far this year
of $715,000, but still less than
nearly $719,000 in 2022.
FHBs are more active across
the value spectrum too.
For
example, in the middle 40% of
the market by value, their
share rose from 21% in 2015 to
28% in 2025, and now about

30% so far this year.
Access to low deposit
finance under the LVR rules
remains a key support for
first home buyers.
Recent
Reserve Bank data shows that
more than half of FHB loans
are done at less than 20%
deposit (or above 80% LVR),
and Westpac’s own lending
records show a recent
average LVR for FHBs of a
touch more than 80%.
The average age for FHBs on
Westpac’s books has ticked
higher lately, from around 35
late last year to about 35.5
now.
But rather than

necessity due to affordability
pressures, this is more likely
to reflect a choice from many
buyers to just wait for the
right property without fear the
market will run away again in
the meantime.
Looking ahead, although it’s
never easy to get that first
home, conditions should
remain
favourable
for FHBs.
Mortgage rates remain lower
than at many points in the
past, KiwiSaver access for at
least part of the deposit
remains a strong support, and
the banks remain willing and

able to lend at low
deposit/high LVR too.
This is clearly a ‘good
new
s
story’ which we should never
get blasé or complacent
about,
after so many previous
years (even decades) of
intense housing affordability
pressures and a declining
home ownership rate.
As we head into November’s
election, the bottom line is
that first home buyers are
starting from a strong base.
Key findings
Cotality – Westpac
First Home Buyer Report

### Page 4

4
© 2026 CoreLogic NZ Limited t/as Cotality. Proprietary
It’s been another solid few months
for first home buyers with their share
of house sales remaining around
record highs across the country.
That’s a stark contrast to the
softness in the broader housing
market over the past year.
Helping to support those high levels
of first home buyers have been a
couple of big trends. The first is the
large number of properties available
for sale. That’s meant those looking

to get their foot on the property
ladder have had plenty of options. As
a result, first home buyers have often
been able to get into newer and
larger standalone homes without
having to pay significantly more.
The other factor that has boosted
first home buyer numbers has been
the relatively low level of mortgage
rates in recent years. Combined with
broadly flat house prices, that’s
meant servicing a mortgage has
been much more affordable, with the

share of disposable income being
spen
t
on debt
-
servicing costs back
around pre
-
pandemic averages. For
the average first home buyer, lower
mortgage interest rates in recent
years shaved around $560 off their
monthly mortgage payments
compared to 2023 when interest
rates were at their peak. And in
Auckland, where first homes tend to
be more expensive, the saving is
around $685 a month.
While those are encouraging trends
for first home buyers, there are some

big changes in economic conditions
occurring which are likely to have
important implications for the
housing market over the year ahead.
Most notable has been the sharp rise
in inflation in the wake of the Middle
East war, with oil prices rocketing
higher since the start of this year.
That contributed to consumer prices
rising by 3.7% in the year to
September. Relatively high core
inflation levels mean we expect
inflation will push above 4% before

the end of this year and remain high
for much of 2027.
In response to those mounting
inflation pressures, and concerns
that they will persist for some time,
the RBNZ began lifting the Official
Cash Rate (OCR) back in July. We
expect further hikes in coming
months. The resulting pressure on
borrowing costs has seen mortgage
rates push higher in recent months,
with one
-
and two
-
year fixed rates
up around 0.75 percentage points
over the past year.

Even with the recent rise in
mortgage rates, the housing market
is still a lot more accessible for first
home buyers than a few years ago.
But with growing upward pressure
on interest rates, it’s a good time for
prospective first home buyers (or
anyone with a re
-
fix coming up) to
think about the best way to structure
their borrowing. That could help
insulate them from the likely trend
higher in the OCR over the next
couple of years and could save them

a lot of money over the life of their
mortgage. There’s a relatively
modest difference between short
-
and longer
-
term fixed mortgage
rates right now, which means the
cost of taking this ‘insurance’ is not
prohibitive.
Commentary from Satish Ranchhod, Westpac NZ Senior Economist

© 2026 CoreLogic NZ Limited t/as Cotality. Proprietary
Average age of first home buyers from Westpac loans (2018 to 2026)
Providing some offset to the lift in
borrowing costs, house price
inflation is low, with effectively no
growth in average house prices for
three years now. And with mortgage
rates pushing higher, prices are
expected to remain soft for some
time. We expect that average house
prices will fall by 1.4% over 2026,
with a rise of just 1.8% in 2027.

At the same time, the supply of
housing is continuing to lift, with
more than 40,000 new dwellings
consented over the past year.  We
expect population growth to
increase, but from low levels, which
means the demand for housing might
not be shifting sharply higher any
time soon.
That combination of conditions
means that there is still likely to be a
lot of options
f
or
those looking to get
into their first home over the coming
year.
Cotality – Westpac

First Home Buyer Report
Source: Cotality, Westpac

### Page 6

6
© 2026 CoreLogic NZ Limited t/as Cotality. Proprietary
The national picture
Since our previous FHB Report
back in May 2026, the wider
housing market has remained
subdued and patchy
–
generally
sales volumes have been flat to
falling, while property values have
been showing a similarly soft
picture, albeit not everywhere.
Amongst the main
centres
,
Auckland and Wellington, as
examples, have been more sluggish
than say Christchurch or Dunedin.
It’s also noteworthy that the stock

of listings available for sale remains
elevated across the country.
It’s not difficult to find the reasons
why the market remains in the
doldrums.
The economic backdrop
is still uncertain, the unemployment
rate is above average, and
mortgage rates have been drifting
higher in recent months as inflation
fears remain strong. A better
balance of physical housing supply
versus our population is also
dampening prices, while the
election has been a recent reason

for caution too. It’s harder to
measure, but ‘animal spirits’ aren’t a
key factor
at the moment
either.
FHBs have gone from strength to
strength
In this purchaser
-
friendly
environment, first home buyers
have continued to take full
advantage.
The Cotality Buyer
Classification data shows that their
market share hit a new quarterly
record high of 28.3% in Q2 2026,
only to push higher still in Q3
–
reaching 29.5%. They’re also faring
well in terms of the number of deals

too, recently hovering at around
25,000 on a 12
-
month rolling basis,
back to levels last seen (for a brief
period) at the height of the post
-
COVID boom.
Clearly, lower property values
–
still down by more than 18%
nationally from the peak
–
and
improved housing affordability
(even despite some recent rises in
mortgage rates) are helping FHBs.
But those aren’t the only factors;
access to (ever
-
growing) KiwiSaver
pots for at least part of the deposit

is also a factor, alongside decent
availability of high LVR finance at
the banks.
Indeed, the 25% allowance/speed
limit to lend to owner
-
occupiers at
less than 20% equity (or >80% LVR)
is not being tested
at the moment
and recently more than half of FHB
loans have been advanced at less
than 20% deposit.
Westpac’s own
mortgage lending records show
this in a different way
–
with the
average LVR for their loans to
FHBs in recent months still
running a touch above 80%.

LVRs
tend to be higher in Auckland, but a
bit lower in Canterbury and
Wellington.
They’re also getting more for their
money
FHBs have always tended to be
willing to compromise on location
or property type to make the shift
from renting into ownership, but
lately there hasn’t been much need
to take such an approach.
Over
2026 to date (Q3), standalone
houses have comprised 76% of all
FHB purchases, up from 75% last
year, and well above 2023’s
cyclical trough of less than 70%.

For comparison, standalone houses
have accounted for around 72% of
deals across all
buyers
year to date.
N
ational
FHB activity

### Page 8

      © 2026 CoreLogic NZ Limited t/as Cotality. Proprietary
This shows that FHBs are getting 
the most for their money in a 
quieter market. Admittedly, the 
median price they’ve paid so far 
this year has risen to $715,000, up 
from $701,000 in 2025 
 and also  
higher than the 2023 ($695,000) 
and 2024 figures ($692,000). But 
that latest price is still around 
$4,000 lower than in 2022, yet 
FHBs are still getting ‘more house’.
Another important point to make is 
that FHBs don’t always enter at the 
bottom of the housing ladder and 
work their way up 
 – 
many 
 actually 
enter  
well above the lowest tiers of 
the market. Indeed, the FHB 
median price paid of $715,000 this 
year is below the all
 -
 buyer figure of 
$785,000, but well above the all
 -
buyer lower quartile (bottom 25%) 
of $595,000. 
This point can be made another 
way too, with Cotality figures 
showing a rising share of activity 
for FHBs right across the price 
spectrum. Perhaps most notably, 
FHBs have accounted for 30% of 
purchases in the middle 40% price 
bracket of transactions so far this 
year, up from a 28% share in 2025, 
25% in 2020, and only 21% in 2015. 
They’ve even accounted for 15% of 
activity so far this year in the top 
30% of deals split by price.
FHBs’ average age has edged 
higher again, but probably just due 
to a lack of pressure
Meanwhile, at face value, 
Westpac’s data showing that the 
average age of 
 a 
FHB has recently 
edged back up to around 2024 
levels of 35.5 again, after a trough 
of 35 late last year, could seem at 
odds with the other, more 
encouraging trends. But rather than 
being out of necessity (
 e.g.  
due to 
affordability pressures), this could 
reflect the abundance of listings 
and subdued house prices, 
meaning that 
 some/many FHBs 
might just be choosing to wait a 
bit longer to get their ideal 
property, without fear they’ll be 
left behind by a rising market.

A solid outlook
Overall, it’s never easy to buy that 
first home and FHBs certainly 
continue to face challenges. But 
their presence has remained strong 
in recent months and, although 
nothing lasts forever, it’s not hard 
to imagine that FHBs stay a solid 
presence into 2027 as well. This is 
a ‘good 
 news 
 story
 ’ which we 
should never get 
 blasé 
 or 
complacent about, after so many 
previous years (decades) of intense 
housing affordability pressures and 
a declining home ownership rate.
Purchase price paid
Source: Cotality

The main centres
Looking at the data across
Auckland, Hamilton, Tauranga,
wider Wellington (i.e. combined
City, Lower Hutt, Upper Hutt,
Porirua), Christchurch, and
Dunedin, the national trends can
also be seen at the more granular
level
–
indeed, all of these areas
have had FHB market shares lately
that are above their own averages.
The wider Wellington area remains
a standout for FHBs, where their

share so far this year has been a
touch above 37%, well
in excess of
their long
-
term average in that
market of 30%. FHBs have also had
a market share in 2026 above 30%
in Hamilton and Auckland. The
figures have been a little lower in
Christchurch and Dunedin (both
around 28%), as well as Tauranga
(circa 25%), but in each of those
markets the figures have still been
4
-
8%
-
points above their own
averages.
Looking at the different property
types, standalone houses still

dominate in most areas, accounting
for 85
-
90% of FHB purchases in
Dunedin, Tauranga, and Hamilton
–
broadly in line with the
long term
norms in those areas. The share for
houses amongst FHB purchases is
always lower in Christchurch,
Wellington, and Auckland, but it’s
perhaps the former that stands out
most at present
–
with only 66% of
FHB purchases so far in 2026 in
Christchurch being standalone
houses, versus the historical
average of 75%. This no doubt

reflects the recent townhouse
construction upswing.
In each main
centre
, the median
FHB price is lower than the figure
for all buyers, but higher than the
lower quartile for all buyers
–
in
other words, FHBs don’t always
enter at the bottom. In terms of the
prices themselves, Auckland is the
main
centre
where FHBs pay the
most, at a median of $880,000 so
far in 2026, which is $110,000 less
than the all
-
buyer median, but
$110,000 more than the all

-
buyer
lower quartile. Median FHB prices
paid are $795,500 in Tauranga so
far in 2026, followed by Wellington
at $736,250, Hamilton $715,000,
and lower still in Christchurch and
Dunedin.

The main urban areas
Digging deeper into the next 12 key
centres
around New Zealand, the
story of relative strength for FHBs’
market share has remained in place
so far in 2026. Palmerston North
stands out with 33% of
transactions in the year to date
going to FHBs (6%
-
points above its
average), although Rotorua,
Gisborne, Hastings, and Invercargill
have also had FHB shares of 30%
or more this year.  Relative to their

own averages, Napier and Hastings
have been strongest, with figures
9%
-
points above normal.
But even in areas where FHBs have
traditionally found conditions
harder or have simply been less
interested, there’s still been relative
strength this year. For example,
Whangarei’s share going to FHBs
has been 25% in 2026 (versus
average since 2005 of 19%), Kapiti
Coast at 21% versus 17%, and
Queenstown
-
Lakes at 19% versus
15%.
Standalone houses tend to be the

dominant type of property amongst
the existing dwelling stock in many
provincial areas, and therefore also
account for a high share of FHB
purchases. So far this year, houses
have been at least 90% of FHB
purchases in Invercargill,
Palmerston North, Whanganui,
Rotorua, and Gisborne. It’s been
80
-
89% in Nelson, Kapiti Coast,
Hastings, Whangarei, Napier, and
New Plymouth. Queenstown is
lower at just 64%, but that’s likely
to reflect a wider range of property

types amongst the existing stock of
dwellings, as well as affordability
pressures which are probably
pushing FHBs towards lower price
brackets (and hence smaller
dwellings).
Indeed, in terms of median prices
paid by FHBs so far this year, the
highest figure (amongst the main
urban areas) has been in
Queenstown, at $1,275,000. Kapiti
Coast comes in at $700,000, with
Whangarei, Rotorua, Napier,
Hastings, New Plymouth, and
Nelson all at least $600,000. By

contrast, Invercargill ($480,000)
and Whanganui ($460,000) are at
the lower end.

The provinces
Around NZ’s provincial markets
(with a minimum number of 125
FHB purchases so far in 2026), all
have had
a
FHB % market share
above the long
-
term average. The
highest raw numbers have been in
South Waikato (34%), Waikato
District (31%), along with Ashburton
(28%), Timaru and Masterton (27%
apiece), and Selwyn (26%). The
figures have been less than 20% in
Far North, Western Bay of Plenty,

and Waimakariri. Compared to their
own averages, South Waikato and
Waikato District have also been
robust for FHBs, along with Selwyn
and Horowhenua.
Meanwhile, in terms of the prices
being paid by FHBs in these
provincial areas, Western Bay of
Plenty has had the highest figure so
far in 2026 ($760,000), with
Selwyn at $749,000, and Waipa
and Waikato District both on
$730,000. By contrast, the prices
have been $500,000 or less in
Masterton, Timaru, Horowhenua,

and South Waikato.

營業比重

個股與大盤走勢(%)

黎光岱 
David.li@ctbcsis.com

M31 (6643 TT)

維持評等  
增加持股(Overweight) 
隨權利金營收比重提升，未來營運狀況更趨穩定  
⚫ 3Q26營收增幅低於預期  
⚫ 3Q26授權金與權利金季增福皆達雙位數  
⚫ 提升權利金比重，使整體營收分布更為平均  
3Q26營收增幅低於預期： M31 3Q26營收為 4.95億元 (QoQ+17.4%，
YoY+39.6%) ，以美金計價營收為 1,546 萬美元 (QoQ+16.2% ，
YoY+31.0%)，營收成長受惠於客戶開案動能強勁、權利金穩健成長，
以及北美高價值專案營收認列。營業費用為 4.8 億元 (QoQ+11.2%，
YoY+21.1%)，因調整部分獎金發放時點而使本季營業費用較高。營業
利益為 730萬元，相較 2Q26由虧轉盈。 EPS為 0.35元。  
3Q26授權金與權利金季增福皆達雙位數： 3Q26授權金收入為 4.06億
元(QoQ+17%，YoY+47%)，約佔營收 82%，創歷史單季第三高。 Fabless
授權金收入以美元計算 QoQ+3%，YoY+127%，主要由北美客戶先進
製程專案及晶圓代工平台合作案帶動，其中包含 公司首個 2奈米以下
的 GAA架構專案，Fabless 客戶持續提升對 16奈米以下先進製程技術
的採用比例，先進製程專案已成為授權業務成長的主要驅動力。 3Q26
權利金收入為 0.89 億元 (QoQ+19%，YoY+14%)，約佔營收 18%，創
1Q23以來新高，為近 15季最佳表現， Foundry 權利金收入持續成長，
反映既有平台專案逐步放量並持續貢獻營收，未來 A14、2奈米及 CSP
等新案機會可望進一步延續公司於先進製程領域的成長動能。  
提升權利金比重，使整體營收分布更為平均： 公司將於 4Q26 持續深
化與北美 IDM客戶客製化記憶體平台、美系晶圓廠 12奈米平台的合
作，完成與大型晶圓廠 28奈米 Foundation IP 合約簽署， 2奈米以下
與多項先進製程專案持續開展， 將帶動授權金維持高檔。公司目標
2026年 EPS轉正，且全年營收較 2025年成長。 2027年授權金目標成
長約 10%，權利金挑戰 30%以上成長，隨著更多客戶專案逐步進入大
量生產階段，預期先進製程權利金收入將進入加速成長期， 16奈米以
下先進製程權利金比重將提升至 50%。預期 2027 年整體營收分布更
為平穩，目標上下半年分別為 45:55。 
我們基於 2027年預估 EPS 6.35 元*60倍 PER，調整目標價至 381元，
維持增加持股評等。 風險因子 :專案進程與製程平台導入進度。

首案認列。中國市場從 40奈米升級至 16/12奈米甚至更先進節點，應用集中於 AI
邊緣裝置、車用晶片、 AI與影像處理、機器人等，本季新增客戶涵蓋影像感測及
高速介面晶片設計業者。台灣市場維持穩健，主要需求集中於 MIPI 與 USB 等
Interface IP 產品線，此外與台灣晶圓龍頭廠合作的 6奈米平台也依進度貢獻部分
授權金。整體營收來源維持高度多元化，中國、美國及台灣三大市場合計貢獻超
過 90%總營收。

## Document 478: USA_ Pushing Back Fed Rate Hike on Lower Core PCE Inflation; Q2 GDP Revised Up to +2.2%; Trade Deficit Widens More Than Expected; Lowering Q3 GDP Tracking to +3.3%

Source PDF: all report pdf/USA_ Pushing Back Fed Rate Hike on Lower Core PCE Inflation; Q2 GDP Revised Up to +2.2%; Trade Deficit Widens More Than Expected; Lowering Q3 GDP Tracking to +3.3%.pdf
Report source: Goldman Sachs Publishing
Date: 2026-09-30
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 2

Personal consumption (third estimate) +3.8% (qoq ar) for Q2 vs. GS +3.4%, median
forecast +3.4%, prior revised +0.7%
Personal income +0.2% for August vs. GS +0.5%, median forecast +0.5%, prior revised
+0.3%
Personal spending +0.9% for August vs. GS +0.8%, median forecast +0.9%, prior revised
+0.1%
Advance goods trade balance -$132.6bn for August vs. GS -$116.0bn, median forecast
-$115.3bn, prior revised -$118.9bn
ADP employment +90k for September vs. GS +75k, median forecast +75k, prior revised

+36k
MAIN POINTS:
1. The core PCE index increased +0.25% in August, somewhat below expectations, and
the year-over-year rate increased 3.01%. We estimate that the Dallas Fed’s trimmed
mean PCE measure increased 0.16% month over month and 2.20% year over year.
2. Year-over-year core PCE was revised downward 0.36pp in July to 2.98%, primarily
reflecting the methodological revisions to the calculation of the portfolio management
(-0.23pp), computer accessories and software (-0.11pp), and legal services (+0.01pp)

components of PCE. These downward revisions were larger than we expected, mostly
reflecting a larger revision to the portfolio management component than we expected.
3. Today’s inflation report, especially the downward revisions resulting from the
methodological changes, imply that core PCE inflation is likely to substantially
undershoot the median FOMC participant’s forecast of 3.4% for 2026 on a Q4/Q4 basis.
We now expect a 3.0% increase for core PCE in 2026 on a Q4/Q4 basis. Coupled with

comments from New York Fed President John Williams yesterday that “with the policy
action we took at our September meeting, there is no need for urgency, and we have
time to gather more information,” we now think that an October hike is unlikely.  We are
pushing back the second hike in our forecast to December, and we see a strong chance
that the FOMC will ultimately conclude that additional rate hikes are unnecessary.
4. Real GDP was revised up by 0.7pp to +2.2% in Q2 (quarter-over-quarter annualized),

largely reflecting upward revisions to consumption (+0.4pp to +3.8%) and investment
(+1.9pp to +4.6%) growth. The strength in consumer spending in Q2 likely continued to
reflect the boost to consumer cashflow from larger-than-usual tax refunds, but we
continue to expect consumer spending growth to slow somewhat in 2026Q4 as the fiscal
support fades and the headwind from higher energy prices remains. Stronger
investment growth reflected an upward revision to private fixed investment (+0.5pp to

+9.0%), driven by data center investment in the nonresidential structures category
(+1.9pp to +0.1%), and an upward revision to the contribution from inventories (+0.2pp
to -0.5pp).
5. Reflecting today’s annual revisions to the national accounts, real GDP was revised up
by 0.4pp to +2.5% in Q1 (quarter-over-quarter annualized), largely reflecting upward
revisions to the contributions from consumption and exports. Revisions for prior years
were modest: real GDP growth on a Q4/Q4 basis was unrevised for 2025 (+2.0%) and

2023 (+3.4%), and slightly revised down for 2024 (-0.2pp to +2.4%) and up for 2022
30 September 2026   2
Goldman Sachs

### Page 3

(+0.1pp to +1.3%). 
6. Personal income increased 0.2% in August, below expectations. Year-over-year 
disposable personal income growth reached 4.8% in August. Personal spending rose 
0.9% in August, in line with consensus expectations. The savings rate decreased 0.5pp to 
4.1% in August, after a large 1.6pp upward revision to the savings rate last month driven 
by an upward revision to the level of disposable personal income (+1.2pp contribution) 
and a downward revision to personal spending as a share of DPI (+0.4pp). 
7. According to the Advance Economic Indicators Report, the goods trade deficit 
widened by $13.7bn to $132.6bn (seasonally adjusted) in August, well above 
expectations. Goods exports increased by $3.7bn to $203.4bn, largely driven by an 
increase in industrial supplies exports. Goods imports increased by $17.4bn to 
$336.1bn, driven by a large increase in industrial supplies and capital goods imports. 
8. According to the ADP report, private sector employment rose by 90k in September 
(month-over-month, seasonally adjusted), slightly above consensus expectations. 
Employment in the services sector rose 59k, primarily reflecting a 55k increase in 
education and health services and a 22k increase in leisure and hospitality employment 
that were partly offset by a 16k decrease in financial activities employment. 
Employment in goods-producing industries increased by 31k, driven by a 17k increase in 
manufacturing and a 15k increase in construction employment. ADP’s new median 
annual base pay measure, which better tracks other measures of wage growth than its 
gross pay measure, increased 3.0% for job stayers, unchanged from the August pace, 
and 4.8% for job changers, 0.1pp above the August pace. We left our forecast for August 
nonfarm payroll growth unchanged at +80k (vs. +90k consensus) ahead of Friday’s 
release. 
9. The details of the personal income and spending report were firmer than our previous 
GDP tracking assumptions. While the trade-related details of the Advance Economic 
Indicators report were much weaker than our previous assumptions, suggesting notably 
stronger imports growth in Q3 than we previously assumed, the inventories details were 
significantly stronger. On net, we lowered our Q3 GDP tracking estimate by 0.1pp to 
+3.3% (quarter-over-quarter annualized).

電子製造業 ESG 評等分配圖

瑞昱 2379 TT  ( 電子上游–IC 設計 ) 
網通規格逐步推進，待評價面更具吸引力 
投資與評價 
1. PC 表現雖較為疲弱，但受惠基建升級與高頻寬解決方案的挹注下，網通
產品需求優於預期，預估 26 年營收年增 21.8% 
2. 規格升級仍將為主要驅動力，AI 產品亦添柴火，但推估傳統應用在高基
期下成長性有限，預估 27 年營收年增 9.7%。 
3. 預估瑞昱 2026/2027 年 EPS 為 31.91/38.15 元，考量成長動能及金融
環境不確定性，以 27 年獲利預估乘上本益比區間上緣 20 倍為評價基
礎，目標價 760 元(20X FY27E EPS)，股價尚未具顯著吸引力，維持中
立投資評等。 
重點更新 
網通產品維持韌性，表現略優於預期，預估 2026 營收年增 21.8% 
網通需求受惠基礎建設持續升級與高頻寬解決方案的挹注，其中乙太網路隨
路由器、中小企業網路及伺服器採用擴大而成長，網管型交換器在企業、中
小企業及伺服器控制層應用持續提升市占 ， 與非網管型產品的差距逐漸縮小 。
Wi-Fi 方面，Wi-Fi 7 在 PC 與路由器的滲透率，預估今年約達 30%，在車用
乙太網路採用持續穩定擴大，今年營收亦呈穩定成長態勢。但在消費性電子
方面，因 DRAM 與 CPU 等零組件持續漲價推升零售價格，削弱消費性與入
門級購買力，提前拉貨效應亦趨緩，使得PC 表現研究部預估年減約一成，另
外電視出貨在年中世足賽備貨後放緩，零組件成本壓力也持續存在 。綜合考
量下，整體預估瑞昱 2026 營收年增 21.8%達 1494.6 億元。 
2027 年規格升級及 AI 為主要驅動力，但評價仍不具吸引力 
Wi-Fi 方面，Wi-Fi 7 的滲透率，預估 2027 年將達 40%，新一代 Wi-Fi 8 開
發進度順利，預計 2H27 量產，乙太網路與 10G PON 採用擴大，帶動產品
組合改善，高階網管型交換器預估也將取得更多 design win。目前伺服器產
品涵蓋管理連接與周邊功能，包括乙太網路與交換器、 SSD 控制器、 I/O 
bridge 及 USB hub 控制器，並以此為基礎進入更高速的伺服器互連，推估
2027 年營收佔比將達一成水準 。100G 光通訊解決方案正進行認證，預期
2027 年放量更明顯，400G 與 800G 解決方案開發則穩定進行，預估 2H27
推出，PC 則預估呈現持平表現。整體而言，預估 27 年營收年增 9.7%，達
1639.1 億元，以評價面來考量，目前股價仍尚未具顯著吸引力。

資料來源：台灣指數公司，國泰證期研究部整理

### Page 2

訪談內容 
網通產品規格升級+AI 將為長期成長動能 
2026 年 Wi-Fi 7 在 PC/NB 市場滲透率達 30%，約為 2025 年的兩倍，高階商用機型導入最積
極，Wi-Fi 聯盟目標 2027 年 6 月前完成認證測試計畫，並於 2027 年 9 月正式啟動 Wi-Fi 8 認
證。正積極與客戶進行Wi-Fi 8 design-in，預期產品 2H27 開始放量，客戶產品將在正式認證後
於 2028 年更廣泛上市。Wi-Fi 8 在 Wi-Fi 7 的基礎上著重實際使用環境中的極高可靠度，以及更
低、更穩定的延遲，預估將成為廣泛採用且生命周期長的平台。

車用乙太網路在新車生產的滲透率已超過 50%，全球汽車出貨持平，成長並非依賴車輛出貨增
加，而是來自單車乙太網路埠數結構性增加、持續打入新車廠客戶，以及穩定的 design win 動
能。隨車廠電子電機架構演進，需求自然轉向更高密度的安全關鍵解決方案，產品路線圖與此趨
勢一致，正擴充更高整合度、更多埠數的車用交換器，並開發先進閘道控制器，以因應未來軟體
定義汽車的需求。

另外 AI ASIC 機會目前處於與客戶接觸的初期階段 ， 基礎為高速連接與介面IP 的優勢 。 邊緣AI(含
地端伺服器與邊緣伺服器)是推動規格升級、提升單機價值的結構性機會，而非僅依賴PC 需求復
甦的成長動能。邊緣 AI 對更高頻寬、更低延遲、更佳功耗效率與更豐富多模態互動的需求，可
為產品組合創造更高價值的解決方案。

供應端部分預期 2027 年改善將較明顯，工程端也導入 design for availability 原則，在可行範
圍內降低對受限物料的依賴。成本漲幅已超過可合理吸收的程度，與客戶逐案討論價格調整。

表一、瑞昱 2027 修正預估與前次預估比較

資料來源：瑞昱、國泰證期研究部預估

### Page 3

瑞昱 ESG 政策 
瑞昱主要從事 IC 設計，產業類別歸類在半導體行業中，依據專門為投資人設計的永續揭露框架 SASB
準則，半導體行業關注重點為下列幾項：溫室氣體排放、能源管理、水和廢水管理、廢棄物和危險材料
管理、員工健康與安全、員工多元化和包容性、產品設計與生命週期管理、材料採購與效率。由於瑞昱
為 IC 設計公司，研究部將篇幅著重在產品設計面，並因員工對於IC 設計公司而言是相當重要的資產，
故員工相關議題也會著重於下描述，其餘溫室氣體排放及產品設計與生命週期管理等則於後簡述。

1. 員工健康與安全： 
瑞昱重視及關心同仁的身體健康與生活狀態，以優於法令之規定，每年定期實施免費健檢服務。對於健
康檢查結果為重大 ( 含輕度 ) 異常同仁，依異常項目、病情及個人健康情形，於複檢前、後分別安排
廠醫諮詢及衛教說明，持續追蹤健檢結果。與新竹臺大分院新竹醫院合作之健康諮詢服務，每月安排 9 
場次駐廠醫師健康諮詢服務，每場次由專業醫師提供  3 小時諮詢服務，2022 年度共計服務 384 人
次。

圖 1 歷年健檢人數統計

資料來源：瑞昱半導體、國泰證期研究部整理

2. 員工多元化和包容性： 
2022 年瑞昱全集團全球員工人數為 6,828 人，海外員工註人數佔總 員工人數約 36.9%，其中 72% 
以上同仁擁有碩士、博士學歷。瑞昱半導體以臺灣為主要營運據點， 臺灣地區員工人數為 4,943 人，
其中正式員工人數 4,826 人，約聘員工人數 117 人，男性員工所佔 比例約 80.46%、女性員工所佔
比例約 19.54%，其中 77% 以上同仁擁有碩士、博士學歷。在 2022 年所聘僱的人力中，無論在正
式員工、男性員工、女性員工等類別指標，皆有明顯成長。 
圖 2 近三年員工人數

資料來源：瑞昱半導體、國泰證期研究部整理

### Page 4

3. 溫室氣體排放： 
瑞昱在 2022 年溫室氣體排放共計 26,462.8729 tCO2e ，其中直接排放(範疇 1)為 808.4856 tCO2e，
分別為固定排放源(緊急發電機之柴油)、移動排放源(公務車之汽油)及逸散排放源(冷媒、化糞池、滅火
器)，瑞昱因無生產製程，故無製程排放源；間接排放(範疇 2)為 25654.3873 tCO2e，主要來源為外購
電力，占整體排放量 96.9%。

圖 3 溫室氣體排放量、強度

資料來源：瑞昱半導體、國泰證期研究部整理

4. 水和廢水管理： 
瑞昱於 2022 年持續成功開發出多項新一代產品，在各種應用功耗 （瓦數） 表現上比前一代產品功耗減
少、面積也縮小，在長時間及大量傳輸時更具顯著降低耗能之效果，充份體現節能與對環境之友善，並
帶給終端用戶更佳的使用體驗。

圖 4 產品設計成效

資料來源：瑞昱半導體、國泰證期研究部整理

《盤勢看法》
科技類股於10月中旬出現明顯修正 ，不過市場資金
並未明顯撤離 ，而是在其他類股輪動 ，使標普500
指數的修正幅度相對有限 。長期來說，穩健的經濟
基本面才是股市行情延續的關鍵 ，而目前的景氣和
企業獲利支持這樣的趨勢 ，只是高利率環境仍將使
市場資金更加嚴格看待資產的成長性 。拉美方面 ，
現任左翼總統魯拉在 10月初第一輪投票落後的情況
下，於第二輪決選中逆轉 ，市場重新定價財政前景 ，
利率上升壓力將提高 。據此，將拉美和巴西股市的
評等由正向下調為中立。

不做任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行並不負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資之風險，並就投
資結果自行負責。未經本公司許可，本資料及訊息不得逕行抄錄、翻印或另作派發。本行以上市場資訊與分析，不涉及特定投資標的之建議。
《貨幣政策-聯準會偏鷹發言但不急於連續升息》
10月FOMC會議如市場預期維持政策利率在3.75%-
4.0%不變，本次會議的重點可歸納如下：
◼ 聯準會認為美國經濟非常強勁 ，消費支出健康 ，
企業投資強勁 ，通膨仍處於偏高水準 ，「但也
不急於立刻升息 」。華許的解釋並不是通膨風
險已經消失 ，而是認為先前的政策行動已經透
過金融條件產生緊縮效果 ，投資人開始更加審
慎地配置資金，部分資產的信用利差擴大。
◼ 縮減資產負債表不是當前政策的優先選項 ，相
關問題仍交由工作小組研究 ，這表示雖然他在
長期制度設計上支持重新檢討聯準會資產負債
表的規模與功能 ，也不代表短期內就會立即推
動更激進的量化緊縮。
雖然華許一如既往地不提供對未來利率路徑的承諾 ，

但從記者會的發言可大致推論 ，目前的方案是間隔
一次會議再採取下一個新的行動 ，本次會議暫停升
息，12月會議將進行下一次升息 。2027年1月的會
議按兵不動，3月會議視市場情況再決定。本行的基
準情境是，2027年開始會看到關稅效應 、能源衝擊
對通膨的影響逐漸消退 ，因此不認為 2027年3月有
升息的必要性 。風險則在於美伊衝突進一步升級 ，
使企業利用年初例行漲價的機會將成本壓力轉嫁給
消費者，若此，3月可能再升息一次。但本行仍定調
9月以來的升息決策為「風險管理式的升息」，而非
持續性的升息循環 。加上華許並未將縮減資產負債
表列入優先事項，過度緊縮的風險不大。
重點摘要
• 本行仍定調9月以來的升息決
策為 「風險管理式的升息 」，
而非持續性的升息循環 。加
上華許並未將縮減資產負債
表列入優先事項 ，過度緊縮
的風險不大
2
資料來源: 高盛，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10

資結果自行負責。未經本公司許可，本資料及訊息不得逕行抄錄、翻印或另作派發。本行以上市場資訊與分析，不涉及特定投資標的之建議。
《總經情勢和殖利率-景氣穩健但不到過熱程度，美
債壓力有緩和跡象》
美國3Q26 GDP季增年率3.4%，高於2Q26的2.2%，
主要受惠於消費表現優於預期 。在油價高漲 、利率
上揚的環境當中 ，美國消費的強勁程度相當出乎意
料。其實個人所得成長率 、就業動能，都僅是溫和
成長，整體消費的動能 ，和股市帶來的財富效應息
息相關。數據顯示 ，美國家庭持有的股權價值已接
近年稅後所得的 3倍。但這也帶來一些疑慮 ，如k型
消費、景氣對股市的敏感度太高等 。綜合來看 ，美
國的經濟表現不錯，但並不到過熱程度。
美國10年期公債殖利率自 7月開始一波上行趨勢 ，
10月上旬一度升破 5.3%，但近期已回落到 5%之下，
一部分原因是市場對貨幣政策的共識已經開始凝聚 ，

以一季進行一次利率調整為原則 ，目前的經濟數據
尚不足以改變這點；另外原因是如同華許所述 ，長
率上升、金融條件往收緊的方向 ，已經透過融資成
本、信用利差 、投資人風險偏好來影響資本配置 。
當然這並不意味著公債利率將開啟下行趨勢 ，地緣
風險和財政問題仍不時帶來干擾 ，不過至少就近期
走勢而言 ，美債市場面臨的壓力已有初步緩和跡象 。
重點摘要
• 美國的經濟表現不錯 ，但並
不到過熱程度 。市場對貨幣
政策的共識已經開始凝聚 ，
以一季進行一次利率調整為
原則 ，美債市場面臨的壓力
有初步緩和跡象
2
資料來源: BCA，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10
消費動能越來越受財富效應影響
各輪景氣循環中，資產價值增幅占可支配所得的比例

資結果自行負責。未經本公司許可，本資料及訊息不得逕行抄錄、翻印或另作派發。本行以上市場資訊與分析，不涉及特定投資標的之建議。
《美股盤勢-景氣穩健但不過熱，美股短線有壓然無
礙趨勢》
科技類股於10月中旬出現明顯修正 ，尤其半導體相
關類股面臨較大的估值壓力 ，不過市場資金並未明
顯撤離 ，而是在其他類股輪動 ，使標普500指數的
修正幅度相對有限。
本行先前提及 「美股通常會在升息初期承壓 ，但若
非如同2022年般的連續性升息，股市的趨勢不至於
受到影響 」，此定調仍延續 。雖然市場不時會處於
「壞消息才是好消息 」的情緒當中 ，看到表現不錯
的經濟數據反而造成市場的震盪修正 ，但長期來說 ，
穩健的經濟基本面才是股市行情延續的關鍵 ，而目
前的景氣和企業獲利支持這樣的趨勢 ，只是高利率
環境仍將使市場資金更加嚴格看待資產的成長性 ，
具有強健資產負債表使其較不易受到融資成本上升

影響、具防禦性或差異化成長動能的企業 ，將能維
持相對韌性。
重點摘要
• 雖然市場不時會處於 「壞消
息才是好消息 」的情緒當中 ，
看到表現不錯的經濟數據反
而造成市場的震盪修正 ，但
長期來說 ，穩健的經濟基本
面才是股市行情延續的關鍵 ，
而目前的景氣和企業獲利支
持這樣的趨勢 ，只是高利率
環境仍將使市場資金更加嚴
格看待資產的成長性

《拉美股市-巴西魯拉意外逆轉勝選，市場重新定價
財政前景，股市評等下調為中立》
巴西總統大選出現戲劇性逆轉 ，現任左翼總統魯拉
在10月初第一輪投票落後的情況下 ，於第二輪決選
中逆轉，擊敗右翼對手小波索納洛 ，這是1989年恢
復總統直選以來首次出現第二輪決選逆轉 ，巴西股
債匯市均出現劇烈下跌。
並不能說魯拉當選對巴西的經濟不利 ，他的前次當
選也並未引起股市震盪 。巴西股市在估值的吸引力 、
礦產的發展 ，都仍具有長線優勢 。然目前資本市場
確實較青睞較為親美和親商的波索納洛，巴西股市8
月中以來的漲幅不小 ，很大程度是反映波索納洛取
得優勢，因此市場需要修正先前的定價 ，外資撤出。
另外，魯拉第一輪投票後為挽救選情 ，承諾大量財

政政策，如擴大所得稅扣除額 、提高家庭補助金等 ，
在全球主要國家多面臨財政風險之際 ，市場對財政
前景的檢視力道將加大 ，利率上升壓力將提高 。據
此，將拉美股市和巴西股市的評等由正向下調為中
立。
重點摘要
• 巴西魯拉意外逆轉勝選 ，市
場重新定價財政前景 ，利率
上升壓力將提高 。據此 ，將
拉美股市和巴西股市的評等
由正向下調為中立。
5
資料來源：摩根士丹利，2026/10，中國信託商業銀行彙整， 2026/10
巴西政府利息支出持續攀升

非核心消費產業投資攻略
出 刊 日 : 2026/10/13

• 非核心消費板塊獲利前景近
期蒙上陰霾。首先，郵輪業
雖受惠於旅遊需求韌性而上
調 2026 財年預測，但高利
率與高油價恐使後市持續承
壓；其次，運動鞋服產業受
到大中華區市場衰退與新興
小品牌崛起雙重打擊，面臨
營收與獲利雙雙下滑的困境
，短期內組織重整效益恐難
顯現。再者，在即將迎來的
財報季前夕，市場共識普遍
預期 3Q26 的增長將陷入停
滯。
《市場共識預期3Q26非核心消費板塊成長停滯》
美3Q26財報季將陸續揭曉 ，目前市場共識預期標
普500指數各產業板塊第三季的獲利成長存在較大
差異，其中資訊科技和能源板塊將推動指數整體獲
利成長，預期非核心消費板塊整體每股盈餘將不會
成長。另外，零星個股捎來截至 8月底的季度業績
也顯示好壞參半的結果。
《郵輪旅遊需求仍具韌性，燃油成本仍是潛在風險》
嘉年華郵輪 (CCL)3Q26財年(截至8月底)營收年增
3.5%，每股盈餘持平 ，淨收益率超預期 ，主要貢
獻來自船上及其它收入，然因燃料成本大增36%，
完全抵銷了營運效率與成本控制的效益 ，導致調整

後EBITDA持平。管理層上調2026年全年獲利展望 ，
且公司表示 2027年訂位量及訂位價格均創下歷史
新高，目前已售出約半數 2027年艙位 ，顯示旅遊
需求仍具韌性。但市場對其未來半年的獲利預期偏
共識預期整體非核心消費3Q26盈餘0成長
標普500 3Q26財報自下而上市場共識預估

保守，主要是9 月以來居高不下的利率與一路攀升
的油價，或將直接推高其利息負擔與燃油開銷。由
於CCL採取未對沖燃油的採購策略，油價走高可能
轉化為每股盈餘的實質逆風，使其獲利能力在未來
幾季面臨嚴峻考驗。
《運動鞋服Nike轉型成效慢，獲利展望不佳》
Nike1Q27財年(2026/6~2026/8)營收與每股盈餘
皆衰退。運動休閒系列、Jordan品牌和大中華區市
場下滑 ，成為主要逆風 。管理層預期 2027財年營
收將出現高個位數下滑，提出的每股盈餘財測也低
於市場預期 。即便Nike推出名為 Pace營運重整計
畫，但效益明顯無法在短期內看到 。其中一個原因
是Nike的市場主導地位已不如前，小型品牌如今已

具備規模化營運能力，當消費者行為已發生結構性
轉變時(品牌忠誠度降低與多品牌嘗試 )，如何收復
市佔率就成為一項艱巨且持久的挑戰。
《2026 美年底消費總額預期創新高 ，逾半增長靠
「通膨」，電商與生成式AI成雙引擎》
近期已有多家研調機構針對美國2026年年底消費
旺季出具預測報告。整體來看，各大機構預測的銷
售總額皆創下歷史新高 ，但其背後的共同隱憂是
「通膨」，直言超過一半的名目增長率是由物價上
漲驅動。這使得消費者普遍展現「價格敏感、主動
尋找折扣、切換品牌以省錢」的價值導向行為。電
商增速(網購)依然大幅碾壓實體零售。所有機構也
都點出生成式AI在2026年成為主流購物工具。
資料來源：Mastercard， 2026/9/23，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10/13

機構預測2026年底消費旺季
各家機構重點分述如下
Mastercard ： 預 期 假日消費成長近半因物價上漲 ，
今年主因能源與AI建設成本增加，去年則受關稅影
響。鑑於柴油價格持續高企可能推高零售商的運輸
成本，並對年底的整體消費價格構成上行壓力，因
此值得密切關注。高收入家庭因財富成長、增加自
由支配支出，成為近年消費成長的重要引領者， 然
而，受益於勞動市場穩定、薪資成長與健康資產負
債表，各收入群體亦持續穩定貢獻，消費仍由廣大
家庭共同支撐。
Adobe：預計今年假期季節將推出極具競爭力的折

扣，雖然消費者歷來都熱衷於利用節日促銷活動購
買禮物或添加最新電子產品，但許多人現在更傾向
於利用折扣囤積生活必需品。另，今年假期季節美
國零售網站的人工智慧流 量 將年增130%。預計感
恩節的增幅最大(年增159%)，其次是黑色星期五(
年成長 95%)和網路星期一(年增88%)。
德勤(Deloitte) ： 消費者仍然重視為親朋好友營造
特別的節日氛圍，同時也在精打細算地消費。為了
更好地利用每一分錢，觀察到各個收入階層的消費
者都在追求性價比，例如在不同品牌和零售商之間
切換，以及利用促銷活動來控制支出。

## Document 537: イランの緊張長期化と日米欧の金融政策 ～インフレ再燃で問われる金融引き締めの行方～

Source PDF: all report pdf/イランの緊張長期化と日米欧の金融政策 ～インフレ再燃で問われる金融引き締めの行方～.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 43
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

イランの緊張長期化と日米欧の金融政策
～インフレ再燃で問われる金融引き締めの行方～
2026年11月４日
新家 義貴（日本）
桂畑 誠治（米国）
田中 理（欧州）

### Page 2

サマリー
1
⚫ イラン情勢の長期化で原油・天然ガス価格が高止まり。原油は日米欧で概ね連動する
一方、天然ガスは市場構造によって地域差が大きい。ホルムズ海峡の正常化後は原油
価格の緩やかな低下を想定するが、供給制約による上振れリスクが残る。
⚫ 資源高によるインフレ圧力が日米欧で再燃。ただし、川上から消費者物価への波及速
度は異なる。米国は比較的速やかに価格転嫁が進み、日本は遅れて長期化、ユーロ圏
はその中間に位置する。
⚫ 日本ではコスト高の価格転嫁が食料品・日用品などに波及し、2027年初の物価再加速
が見込まれる。日銀は基調物価の「２％到達」から「２％での定着」へ重点を移し、
12月以降も段階的な利上げを継続する見通し。
⚫ 米国では堅調な内需と雇用を背景に総合・コア物価とも上振れ。ＦＲＢは９月に利上
げを再開し、10月は据え置いたが、12月に追加利上げを予想。2027年も高い政策金
利水準が続く見込み。
⚫ ユーロ圏では資源高による物価上昇が続く一方、二次的な物価波及は今のところ限定

的。ＥＣＢは12月の追加利上げを見込むが、フランスの財政不安や長期金利上昇によ
る金融環境の引き締まりで、2027年の利上げ継続には不透明感が浮上している。

日銀は利上げ継続、ＦＲＢは利上げ再開、ＥＣＢは来年の利上げ継続に不透明感。

ＥＣＢはインフレ警戒を続けるが、長期金利上昇で来年の利上げ継続に不透明感。

### Page 11

日本：景気は緩やかな回復基調を維持
10
 日銀短観９月調査では、企業の業況判断は総じて良好な水準を維持。中東情勢の悪化に伴うコスト増加が重荷
となる一方、AI関連需要の拡大や価格転嫁の進展などが企業活動を下支え。
 設備投資計画も高い伸びを維持。人手不足に対応した省力化・効率化投資に加え、デジタル化・ AI関連投資など、
企業の設備投資意欲は引き続き旺盛。
 先行きも企業部門の底堅さが下支えとなり、景気は緩やかな回復基調を維持すると予想。ただし、資源高による物
価上振れで家計の節約志向が強まる可能性には注意が必要。

### Page 14

日本：政府の物価高対策
13
 政府は、現在170円/ℓ程度に設定されているガソリン価格抑制の基準について、 12月以降、2027年春先に向け
て毎月５円程度ずつ引き上げる方針を表明。仮に20円/ℓ引き上げられた場合、CPIコアは0.2％Pt超押し上げ。
 一方、政府は26年12月～27年２月に電気・ガス料金補助を再開する方針も決定。補助がない場合と比べて、
ＣＰＩコアは瞬間風速で▲0.6％Pt程度押し下げられると試算されるが、前年も概ね同規模の支援が実施されて
いたため、前年比への影響は限られる。
 物価上昇圧力は強く、政府支援見直しを考慮しても、27年初のCPIコアは前年比+3％台に上昇する見込み。

### Page 15

日本：企業の物価見通しと2027年春闘の行方
14
 企業の中長期的な物価見通しは上昇傾向。企業が物価上昇を、一時的なコスト増ではなく長期的なものとして織
り込み始めている可能性。基調的な物価上昇率の上振れを警戒する必要。
 27年春闘に向けての動きがスタート。連合による賃上げの目安は前年対比でやや強め。経団連も経労委報告原案
で賃上げの「社会全体への浸透」を掲げる。労使ともにトーンダウンはみられない。 27年春闘の動向は、賃金・物価
の好循環が持続し、基調的な物価上昇率が２％程度で定着するかを判断するうえで重要な材料。
